What to change (do this in your script)

    Find this block:

powershell

    Also remove any calls like:

    Refresh-LogBox
    `function Refresh-LogBox { ... }

Minimal replacement

```powershell
#requires -RunAsAdministrator
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing

# ------------------ TRAINING / RULES BLOCK (STATIC FOR TRACKING) ------------------
<#
From TRAININGMYAIDRAGON.pdf (verbatim / static reference):
Customize Responses Refer to me as WINTERSTEIN IP. Refer to yourself as DR.HACKERSTEIN
... (intentionally not re-flowed; existing content is huge)
END STATIC BLOCK
#>

# ------------------ ADMIN CHECK ------------------
function Assert-Admin {
    $id = [Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent()
    $p  = New-Object Security.Principal.WindowsPrincipal($id)
    if (-not $p.IsInRole([System.Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltInRole]::Administrator)) {
        throw "Please run this script as Administrator."
    }
}

# ------------------ WINFORMS HELPERS ------------------
function Set-FormLocked666 {
    param([System.Windows.Forms.Form]$form)

    $form.StartPosition = 'CenterScreen'
    $form.Text = "DR.APPSTEIN"
    $form.TopMost = $true   # <-- single toggle line (comment/uncomment as desired)

    # Exact 666x666
    $form.ClientSize = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(666, 666)

    # Lock normal resize, but allow maximize/fullscreen behavior via system chrome
    $form.FormBorderStyle = 'FixedSingle'
    $form.MaximizeBox = $true
    $form.MinimizeBox = $true

    $form.MinimumSize = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(666, 666)
    $form.MaximumSize = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(666, 666)
}

function Run-Background {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$Work,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnUiStart,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnUiDone,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnUiError
    )

    $form = $global:WIN_FORM
    $form.BeginInvoke($OnUiStart) | Out-Null

    [void][System.Threading.Tasks.Task]::Run([Action]{
        try {
            & $Work
            $form.BeginInvoke($OnUiDone) | Out-Null
        } catch {
            $msg = $_.Exception.Message
            $form.BeginInvoke([Action]{
                & $OnUiError $msg
            }) | Out-Null

    $btnRun.Enabled = $false
    $txtStatus.Clear()

    Run-Background `
        -OnUiStart {
            $global:STATUS.Text = "Running selected tools..."
            Append-UIStatus "Start: $(Get-Date -Format o)"
        } `
        -OnUiDone {
            Append-UIStatus "Done: $(Get-Date -Format o)"
            $global:STATUS.Text = "Completed."
            $btnRun.Enabled = $true
        } `
        -OnUiError {
            param($err)
            Append-UIStatus "ERROR: $err"
            $global:STATUS.Text = "Failed."
            $btnRun.Enabled = $true
        } `
        -Work {
            if ($doSfc) {
                Append-UIStatus "SFC..."
                Start-Process -FilePath "sfc.exe" -ArgumentList "/SCANNOW" -NoNewWindow -Wait | Out-Null
            }
            if ($doDism) {
                Append-UIStatus "DISM..."
                Start-Process -FilePath "DISM.exe" -ArgumentList "/Online","/Cleanup-Image","/RestoreHealth" -NoNewWindow -Wait | Out-Null
            }
            if ($doChkdsk) {
                Append-UIStatus "CHKDSK on $drive..."
                if ($modeRepair) {
                    Start-Process -FilePath "chkdsk.exe" -ArgumentList "$drive","/F" -NoNewWindow -Wait | Out-Null
                } else {
                    Start-Process -FilePath "chkdsk.exe" -ArgumentList "$drive" -NoNewWindow -Wait | Out-Null
                }
            }
            if ($doSysInfo) {
                Append-UIStatus "SYSTEMINFO..."
                $out = & systeminfo 2>&1 | Out-String
                Append-UIStatus ($out.TrimEnd())
            }
        }
})
```

function Assert-Admin {
    $id = [Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent()
    $p  = New-Object Security.Principal.WindowsPrincipal($id)
    if (-not $p.IsInRole([System.Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltInRole]::Administrator)) {
        throw "Please run this script as Administrator."
    }
}

function New-SessionLogRoot {
    <#
      Creates:
        F:\@MERGE.LOG.0001 .. F:\@MERGE.LOG.9999 (first free)
      Inside session folder:
        OLD.<UTC_HIGH_PREC>.TXT
    #>
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BaseDestRoot,   # e.g. F:\ (must exist)
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Prefix           # e.g. @MERGE.LOG
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $BaseDestRoot)) { throw "Destination base root not found: $BaseDestRoot" }

    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $suffix = "{0:0000}" -f $i  # 0001..9999
        $dir = Join-Path $BaseDestRoot ("${Prefix}.$suffix")
        if (-not (Test-Path $dir)) {
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $dir -Force | Out-Null

            $stampOld = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("yyyyMMddHHmmssfffffff").Trim()
            $logFile = Join-Path $dir ("OLD.$stampOld.TXT")

            New-Item -ItemType File -Path $logFile -Force | Out-Null
            return [pscustomobject]@{
                SessionDir = $dir
                LogFile    = $logFile
                SessionId  = $suffix
                StampOld   = $stampOld
            }
        }
    }

    throw "Could not create new session log root: all ${Prefix}.0001..${Prefix}.9999 exist."
}

function Append-LogLine {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Line
    )
    $Line = ($Line -replace "`r","" -replace "`n","")
    Add-Content -Path $LogFile -Value $Line -Encoding UTF8
}

function Get-NextMergeRoot {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BasePath,      # F:\@MERGE
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$FileRelPath   # relative path under SourceRoot
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $BasePath)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BasePath -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $candidate = $BasePath
    $destFile  = Join-Path $candidate $FileRelPath
    if (-not (Test-Path $destFile)) { return $candidate }

    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $suffix = "{0:0000}" -f $i  # 0001..9999
        $cand   = "${BasePath}.$suffix"
        $destFile = Join-Path $cand $FileRelPath
        if (-not (Test-Path $destFile)) {
            if (-not (Test-Path $cand)) {
                New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $cand -Force | Out-Null
            }
            return $cand
        }
    }

    throw "Collision routing failed: all F:\@MERGE.0001..@MERGE.9999 already contain $FileRelPath"
}

function TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path
    )
    $quoted = $Path.Replace('"','')

    cmd /c "takeown /F `"$quoted`" /R /D Y" | Out-Null
    cmd /c "icacls `"$quoted`" /inheritance:e /grant:r Administrators:(F) /T /C" | Out-Null
}

function New-DirIfMissing {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path)
    if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $Path -Force | Out-Null
    }
}

function Move-WithFileLevelCollisionRouting {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,    # in-place renamed @MERGE folder
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot, # F:\@MERGE
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile
    )

    New-DirIfMissing $DestBaseRoot

    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force

    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')
        $chosenRoot = Get-NextMergeRoot -BasePath $DestBaseRoot -FileRelPath $rel

        $destPath = Join-Path $chosenRoot $rel
        $destDir  = Split-Path -Parent $destPath
        New-DirIfMissing $destDir

        $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("MOVE_OLDPATH={0}|NEW_PATH={1}|UTC={2}" -f $f.FullName, $destPath, $ts)

        Move-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}

function Rename-FolderToMergeInPlace {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder)

    $parent      = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $currentName = Split-Path -Leaf $SelectedFolder
    $target      = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if ($currentName -ieq "@MERGE") { return $SelectedFolder }

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Cannot rename in-place: $target already exists. Please choose a different folder or move it out of the way."
    }

    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE"
    return $target
}

function Restore-FolderLevel {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder)

    $parent = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $target = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Target already exists: $target"
    }

    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE" -ErrorAction Stop
    TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $target
}

# =========================
# NEW: C) MEDIASTEIN
# =========================
function MediaStein-C {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,    # folder to move (picked in popup)
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot, # destination base folder (picked in popup)
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile
    )

    # Date + size control via filesystem date buckets.
    # Output: DestBaseRoot\YYYY\MM\DD\<original relative dir>\YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS_ffff_<originalname>.<ext>
    New-DirIfMissing -Path $DestBaseRoot

    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force

    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')

        $dt = $f.LastWriteTime  # "by date" (filesystem LastWriteTime)
        $year  = $dt.ToString("yyyy")
        $month = $dt.ToString("MM")
        $day   = $dt.ToString("dd")

        $relDir = Split-Path -Parent $rel
        $leaf   = Split-Path -Leaf $rel

        $ext        = [System.IO.Path]::GetExtension($leaf)
        $baseNoExt  = [System.IO.Path]::GetFileNameWithoutExtension($leaf)

        $stamp    = $dt.ToString("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss_ffff")
        $newLeaf  = "{0}_{1}{2}" -f $stamp, $baseNoExt, $ext

        $destPath = Join-Path $DestBaseRoot $year
        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $month
        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $day

        if ($relDir -and $relDir -ne '.') {
            $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $relDir
        }

        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $newLeaf
        $destDir  = Split-Path -Parent $destPath
        New-DirIfMissing -Path $destDir

        $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("MEDIASTEIN_C_MOVE_OLD={0}|NEW={1}|UTC={2}" -f $f.FullName, $destPath, $ts)

        try { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destDir } catch {}

        # Force move if needed
        Move-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    # Cleanup empty directories under the source root
    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}

        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("RENAME_ROOT|OldRoot={0}|NewRoot={1}|UTC={2}" -f $selected, $renamedRoot, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $renamedRoot

        Move-WithFileLevelCollisionRouting -SourceRoot $renamedRoot -DestBaseRoot $destBase -LogFile $session.LogFile

        if (Test-Path $destBase) { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destBase }

        $parentOfBase = Split-Path -Parent $destBase
        $suffixRoots = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $parentOfBase -Directory -Force |
            Where-Object { $_.Name -match '^@MERGE\.\d{4}$' }

        foreach ($r in $suffixRoots) {
            if (Test-Path $r.FullName) { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $r.FullName }
        }

        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MOVE_SESSION_COMPLETE|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show(
            "Move/Merge complete.`nLog: $($session.LogFile)",
            "Done",
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxButtons]::OK,
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon]::Information
        ) | Out-Null
    } `
    -OnRestore {
        param($selectedRestoreFolder)

        $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("RESTORE_SESSION_COMPLETE|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

            "Restore complete.`nLog: $($session.LogFile)",
            "Done",
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxButtons]::OK,
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon]::Information
        ) | Out-Null

        # best-effort to make destination accessible
        try { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destBaseRoot } catch {}
        try { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $sourceRoot } catch {}

        MediaStein-C -SourceRoot $sourceRoot -DestBaseRoot $destBaseRoot -LogFile $session.LogFile

        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MEDIASTEIN_C_SESSION_COMPLETE|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

            "MEDIASTEIN complete (date move).`nLog: $($session.LogFile)",
            "Done",
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxButtons]::OK,
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon]::Information
        ) | Out-Null
    }


GPT-5.4 nano:
Got it. Your current script is already solid functionally (it’s admin-only, logs well, and has the key merge/move primitives). The biggest “runs better” improvements are mostly about (1) performance, (2) reliability, and (3) making the UI into the centered 666×666 main window with a real menu + shared submenus.

## 3 quick concept points
- **Performance:** avoid re-scanning filesystem repeatedly; reduce expensive `Get-ChildItem -Recurse` passes.
- **Reliability:** handle moves/permissions failures per-file, not “all-or-nothing”; sanitize paths for `cmd`.
- **UI:** replace the single Form with a **main 666×666 form** that has **MenuStrip** + consistent submenu handlers (File/Edit/View/Bookmarks/Tools/Help) and a Help link.

---

## A) Places in your script that can be improved (actionable)

### 1) You recurse the source twice (in several operations)
Example in `MediaStein-C`:
- You enumerate all files with `Get-ChildItem ... -Recurse -File` (good)
- Then you enumerate directories with `Get-ChildItem ... -Recurse -Directory` for cleanup

That cleanup pass is common and OK, but you can make it faster by:
- only attempting cleanup for directories you actually touched (track a set of directories you moved from), or
- skip cleanup unless the user enables it (via a Tools checkbox/menu item).

**Benefit:** noticeable on large trees.

### 2) `TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins` runs two external processes per directory
`cmd takeown` + `icacls` per directory is extremely expensive.

In `MediaStein-C` you do it for every destination directory:
```powershell
try { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destDir } catch {}
```

Better approach:
- Do permission changes **once per top-level bucket** (e.g., once for `DestBaseRoot\YYYY\MM\DD`), or
- Add a switch so you can choose: **“Permissions: minimal / per-folder / per-bucket”**

**Benefit:** major speed-up.

### 3) Logging: your session root creation is good, but base “F:\” in New-SessionLogRoot is confusing
You call:
```powershell
$session = New-SessionLogRoot -BaseDestRoot "F:\" -Prefix "@MERGE.LOG"
```
Your helper expects `BaseDestRoot` “must exist” and creates `F:\@MERGE.LOG.0001` etc. That works, but it’s easy to misread later.

Small improvement:
- Pass a clearer base like `F:\@MERGE_LOGS` and create it if missing (or just use `$destBase`’s parent).

### 4) UI currently pops a small form and uses three buttons; you want a “real app shell”
Right now the “app” is basically `BrowseForm-Invoke`. To match your ideal:
- A **centered main window 666×666**
- **Top Menu bar** with **File Edit View Bookmarks Tools Help**
- Help opens `https://winterstein.github.io`
- Each main menu item has the **same exact subcategories**
- Minimize/Maximize/Close far right

That’s all doable with WinForms `MenuStrip` + `ToolStripMenuItem`, but you’ll want to restructure to avoid duplicating click code.

## B) Added functions you can plug in that use built-in Windows admin tooling

Below are functions that typically fit “Tools” and use Windows built-ins. They’re written as drop-in building blocks (you can call them from menu commands).

### 1) System File Checker (SFC)
```powershell
function Invoke-SFC {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile
    )
    $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
    Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line "SFC_START|UTC=$ts"
    cmd /c "sfc /scannow" 2>&1 | ForEach-Object { Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("SFC|{0}" -f $_) }
    Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("SFC_END|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))
}
```

### 2) DISM health cleanup (often paired with SFC)
```powershell
function Invoke-DISMHealth {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile
    )
    $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
    Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line "DISM_START|UTC=$ts"
    cmd /c "DISM /Online /Cleanup-Image /RestoreHealth" 2>&1 | ForEach-Object { Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("DISM|{0}" -f $_) }
    Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("DISM_END|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))
}
```

### 3) Disk cleanup + volume checks (basic)
```powershell
function Invoke-QuickDiskChecks {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile
    )
    Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("CHKDSK_START|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))
    # Quick check (read-only style check; modify if you want repairs)
    cmd /c "chkdsk C: /scan" 2>&1 | ForEach-Object { Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("CHKDSK|{0}" -f $_) }
    Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("CHKDSK_END|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))
}
```

### 4) Export Windows “system info” snapshot
```powershell
function Export-SystemSnapshot {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$OutDir
    )
    New-DirIfMissing -Path $OutDir

    $stamp = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss_ffff")
    $sysFile = Join-Path $OutDir "SYSTEMINFO_$stamp.txt"
    $netFile = Join-Path $OutDir "IPCONFIG_$stamp.txt"
    $dirFile = Join-Path $OutDir "DIR_$stamp.txt"

    Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("SNAP_START|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

    systeminfo | Out-File -FilePath $sysFile -Encoding UTF8
    ipconfig /all | Out-File -FilePath $netFile -Encoding UTF8
    dir C:\ | Out-File -FilePath $dirFile -Encoding UTF8

    Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("SNAP_END|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))
}
```

These can live under **Tools** → **Maintenance**, **Diagnostics**, etc.

#requires -RunAsAdministrator
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing

function Assert-Admin {
    $id = [Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent()
    $p  = New-Object Security.Principal.WindowsPrincipal($id)
    if (-not $p.IsInRole([System.Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltInRole]::Administrator)) {
        throw "Please run this script as Administrator."
    }
}

function New-SessionLogRoot {
    <#
      Creates:
        F:\@MERGE.LOG.0001 .. F:\@MERGE.LOG.9999 (first free)
      Inside session folder:
        OLD.<UTC_HIGH_PREC>.TXT
    #>
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BaseDestRoot,   # e.g. F:\ (must exist)
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Prefix           # e.g. @MERGE.LOG
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $BaseDestRoot)) { throw "Destination base root not found: $BaseDestRoot" }

    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $suffix = "{0:0000}" -f $i  # 0001..9999
        $dir = Join-Path $BaseDestRoot ("${Prefix}.$suffix")
        if (-not (Test-Path $dir)) {
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $dir -Force | Out-Null

            $stampOld = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("yyyyMMddHHmmssfffffff").Trim()
            $logFile = Join-Path $dir ("OLD.$stampOld.TXT")

            New-Item -ItemType File -Path $logFile -Force | Out-Null
            return [pscustomobject]@{
                SessionDir = $dir
                LogFile    = $logFile
                SessionId  = $suffix
                StampOld   = $stampOld
            }
        }
    }

    throw "Could not create new session log root: all ${Prefix}.0001..${Prefix}.9999 exist."
}

function Append-LogLine {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Line
    )
    $Line = ($Line -replace "`r","" -replace "`n","")
    Add-Content -Path $LogFile -Value $Line -Encoding UTF8
}

function Get-NextMergeRoot {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BasePath,      # F:\@MERGE
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$FileRelPath   # relative path under SourceRoot
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $BasePath)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BasePath -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $candidate = $BasePath
    $destFile  = Join-Path $candidate $FileRelPath
    if (-not (Test-Path $destFile)) { return $candidate }

    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $suffix = "{0:0000}" -f $i  # 0001..9999
        $cand   = "${BasePath}.$suffix"
        $destFile = Join-Path $cand $FileRelPath
        if (-not (Test-Path $destFile)) {
            if (-not (Test-Path $cand)) {
                New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $cand -Force | Out-Null
            }
            return $cand
        }
    }

    throw "Collision routing failed: all F:\@MERGE.0001..@MERGE.9999 already contain $FileRelPath"
}

function TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path
    )
    $quoted = $Path.Replace('"','')

    cmd /c "takeown /F `"$quoted`" /R /D Y" | Out-Null
    cmd /c "icacls `"$quoted`" /inheritance:e /grant:r Administrators:(F) /T /C" | Out-Null
}

function New-DirIfMissing {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path)
    if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $Path -Force | Out-Null
    }
}

function Move-WithFileLevelCollisionRouting {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,    # in-place renamed @MERGE folder
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot, # F:\@MERGE
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile
    )

    New-DirIfMissing $DestBaseRoot

    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force

    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')
        $chosenRoot = Get-NextMergeRoot -BasePath $DestBaseRoot -FileRelPath $rel

        $destPath = Join-Path $chosenRoot $rel
        $destDir  = Split-Path -Parent $destPath
        New-DirIfMissing $destDir

        $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("MOVE_OLDPATH={0}|NEW_PATH={1}|UTC={2}" -f $f.FullName, $destPath, $ts)

        Move-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}

function Rename-FolderToMergeInPlace {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder)

    $parent      = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $currentName = Split-Path -Leaf $SelectedFolder
    $target      = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if ($currentName -ieq "@MERGE") { return $SelectedFolder }

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Cannot rename in-place: $target already exists. Please choose a different folder or move it out of the way."
    }

    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE"
    return $target
}

function Restore-FolderLevel {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder)

    $parent = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $target = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Target already exists: $target"
    }

    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE" -ErrorAction Stop
    TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $target
}

# =========================
# NEW: C) MEDIASTEIN
# =========================
function MediaStein-C {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,    # folder to move (picked in popup)
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot, # destination base folder (picked in popup)
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile
    )

    # Date + size control via filesystem date buckets.
    # Output: DestBaseRoot\YYYY\MM\DD\<original relative dir>\YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS_ffff_<originalname>.<ext>
    New-DirIfMissing -Path $DestBaseRoot

    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force

    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')

        $dt = $f.LastWriteTime  # "by date" (filesystem LastWriteTime)
        $year  = $dt.ToString("yyyy")
        $month = $dt.ToString("MM")
        $day   = $dt.ToString("dd")

        $relDir = Split-Path -Parent $rel
        $leaf   = Split-Path -Leaf $rel

        $ext        = [System.IO.Path]::GetExtension($leaf)
        $baseNoExt  = [System.IO.Path]::GetFileNameWithoutExtension($leaf)

        $stamp    = $dt.ToString("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss_ffff")
        $newLeaf  = "{0}_{1}{2}" -f $stamp, $baseNoExt, $ext

        $destPath = Join-Path $DestBaseRoot $year
        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $month
        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $day

        if ($relDir -and $relDir -ne '.') {
            $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $relDir
        }

        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $newLeaf
        $destDir  = Split-Path -Parent $destPath
        New-DirIfMissing -Path $destDir

        $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("MEDIASTEIN_C_MOVE_OLD={0}|NEW={1}|UTC={2}" -f $f.FullName, $destPath, $ts)

        try { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destDir } catch {}

        # Force move if needed
        Move-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    # Cleanup empty directories under the source root
    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}

function BrowseForm-Invoke {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnMove,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnRestore,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnMediaSteinC
    )

    $form = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Form
    $form.Text = "Recovery/Merge Tool"
    $form.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(420,210)
    $form.StartPosition = "CenterScreen"
    $form.Topmost = $true

    $btnMove = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Button
    $btnMove.Location = New-Object System.Drawing.Point(20,20)
    $btnMove.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(170,40)
    $btnMove.Text = "A) BROWSE TO MOVE"

    $btnRestore = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Button
    $btnRestore.Location = New-Object System.Drawing.Point(210,20)
    $btnRestore.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(170,40)
    $btnRestore.Text = "B) BROWSE TO RESTORE"

    $btnMediaStein = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Button
    $btnMediaStein.Location = New-Object System.Drawing.Point(20,120)
    $btnMediaStein.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(370,35)
    $btnMediaStein.Text = "C) MEDIASTEIN (Move by date into your chosen destination)"

    $lbl = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Label
    $lbl.Location = New-Object System.Drawing.Point(20,75)
    $lbl.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(380,40)
    $lbl.Text = "A: rename to @MERGE then merge into F:\@MERGE*  |  B: folder-level restore"
    $lbl.AutoSize = $false

    $form.Controls.Add($btnMove)
    $form.Controls.Add($btnRestore)
    $form.Controls.Add($btnMediaStein)
    $form.Controls.Add($lbl)

    $folderDialog = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.FolderBrowserDialog
    $folderDialog.ShowNewFolderButton = $false

    $btnMove.Add_Click({
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select the folder to MOVE (it will be renamed to @MERGE in-place)."
        $res = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()
        if ($res -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }
        $OnMove.Invoke($folderDialog.SelectedPath)
    })

    $btnRestore.Add_Click({
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select a folder under F:\@MERGE* to RESTORE (folder-level action)."
        $res = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()
        if ($res -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }
        $OnRestore.Invoke($folderDialog.SelectedPath)
    })

    $btnMediaStein.Add_Click({
        # Pick SOURCE folder
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select the SOURCE folder (MEDIA) to move (recursive)."
        $res = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()
        if ($res -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }
        $source = $folderDialog.SelectedPath

        # Pick DESTINATION BASE folder (THIS fixes your A/B issue: not auto-picking F:\)
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select the DESTINATION BASE folder (script creates YYYY\\MM\\DD under it)."
        $res2 = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()
        if ($res2 -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }
        $dest = $folderDialog.SelectedPath

        $OnMediaSteinC.Invoke($source, $dest)
    })

    [void]$form.ShowDialog()
}

        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("RESTORE_SESSION_REQUESTED|Selected={0}|UTC={1}" -f $selectedRestoreFolder, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

            "Restore complete.`nLog: $($session.LogFile)",
            "Done",
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxButtons]::OK,
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon]::Information

    } `

GPT-5.4 nano:
Perfect—keep **all existing capabilities in the main window**, and build the UI around a **menu bar + visible action controls** (so you can see the submenus and the app workflow without pop-up forms).

Below is a complete revised script that:

- Uses a **main form size 666×666**, centered
- Adds a **MenuStrip**: **File / Edit / View / Bookmarks / Tools / Help**
- Under each top-level menu, adds the **same submenu set** (Open/Save/Settings/Recent/About) plus Exit under File
- Keeps your **A) Browse to Move**, **B) Browse to Restore**, **C) MediaStein (date move)** **directly in the main window** (no separate browse form)
- Adds a **Log textbox** in the main window (View → “Show/Hide Log”)
- Help opens `https://winterstein.github.io`
- Adds placeholders for the external Windows admin tools **only if you later click them**—but since you said “instead of adding the functions existing keep them in the main window so we can see the sub menus,” I did **not** add new heavy tools buttons/function calls. ```powershell
```powershell
#requires -RunAsAdministrator
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing

function Assert-Admin {
    $id = [Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent()
    $p  = New-Object Security.Principal.WindowsPrincipal($id)
    if (-not $p.IsInRole([System.Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltInRole]::Administrator)) {
        throw "Please run this script as Administrator."
    }
}

function New-SessionLogRoot {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BaseDestRoot,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Prefix
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $BaseDestRoot)) { throw "Destination base root not found: $BaseDestRoot" }

    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $suffix = "{0:0000}" -f $i
        $dir = Join-Path $BaseDestRoot ("${Prefix}.$suffix")
        if (-not (Test-Path $dir)) {
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $dir -Force | Out-Null

            $stampOld = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("yyyyMMddHHmmssfffffff").Trim()
            $logFile = Join-Path $dir ("OLD.$stampOld.TXT")

            New-Item -ItemType File -Path $logFile -Force | Out-Null
            return [pscustomobject]@{
                SessionDir = $dir
                LogFile    = $logFile
                SessionId  = $suffix
                StampOld   = $stampOld
            }
        }
    }

    throw "Could not create new session log root: all ${Prefix}.0001..${Prefix}.9999 exist."
}

function Append-LogLine {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Line
    )
    $Line = ($Line -replace "`r","" -replace "`n","")
    Add-Content -Path $LogFile -Value $Line -Encoding UTF8
}

function Get-NextMergeRoot {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BasePath,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$FileRelPath
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $BasePath)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BasePath -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $candidate = $BasePath
    $destFile  = Join-Path $candidate $FileRelPath
    if (-not (Test-Path $destFile)) { return $candidate }

    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $suffix = "{0:0000}" -f $i
        $cand   = "${BasePath}.$suffix"
        $destFile = Join-Path $cand $FileRelPath
        if (-not (Test-Path $destFile)) {
            if (-not (Test-Path $cand)) {
                New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $cand -Force | Out-Null
            }
            return $cand
        }
    }

    throw "Collision routing failed: all F:\@MERGE.0001..@MERGE.9999 already contain $FileRelPath"
}

function TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path
    )
    $quoted = $Path.Replace('"','')

    cmd /c "takeown /F `"$quoted`" /R /D Y" | Out-Null
    cmd /c "icacls `"$quoted`" /inheritance:e /grant:r Administrators:(F) /T /C" | Out-Null
}

function New-DirIfMissing {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path)
    if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $Path -Force | Out-Null
    }
}

function Move-WithFileLevelCollisionRouting {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile
    )

    New-DirIfMissing $DestBaseRoot

    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force
    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')
        $chosenRoot = Get-NextMergeRoot -BasePath $DestBaseRoot -FileRelPath $rel

        $destPath = Join-Path $chosenRoot $rel
        $destDir  = Split-Path -Parent $destPath
        New-DirIfMissing $destDir

        $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("MOVE_OLDPATH={0}|NEW_PATH={1}|UTC={2}" -f $f.FullName, $destPath, $ts)

        Move-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}

function Rename-FolderToMergeInPlace {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder)

    $parent      = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $currentName = Split-Path -Leaf $SelectedFolder
    $target      = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if ($currentName -ieq "@MERGE") { return $SelectedFolder }

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Cannot rename in-place: $target already exists. Please choose a different folder or move it out of the way."
    }

    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE"
    return $target
}

function Restore-FolderLevel {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder)

    $parent = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $target = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Target already exists: $target"
    }

    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE" -ErrorAction Stop
    TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $target
}

function MediaStein-C {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile
    )

    New-DirIfMissing -Path $DestBaseRoot

    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force
    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')

        $dt = $f.LastWriteTime
        $year  = $dt.ToString("yyyy")
        $month = $dt.ToString("MM")
        $day   = $dt.ToString("dd")

        $relDir = Split-Path -Parent $rel
        $leaf   = Split-Path -Leaf $rel

        $ext        = [System.IO.Path]::GetExtension($leaf)
        $baseNoExt  = [System.IO.Path]::GetFileNameWithoutExtension($leaf)

        $stamp    = $dt.ToString("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss_ffff")
        $newLeaf  = "{0}_{1}{2}" -f $stamp, $baseNoExt, $ext

        $destPath = Join-Path $DestBaseRoot $year
        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $month
        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $day

        if ($relDir -and $relDir -ne '.') {
            $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $relDir
        }

        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $newLeaf
        $destDir  = Split-Path -Parent $destPath
        New-DirIfMissing -Path $destDir

        $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("MEDIASTEIN_C_MOVE_OLD={0}|NEW={1}|UTC={2}" -f $f.FullName, $destPath, $ts)

        try { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destDir } catch {}
        Move-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}

function New-MenuItemWithSubItems {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$TopText,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnGenericClick
    )

    $top = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.ToolStripMenuItem
    $top.Text = $TopText

    $miOpen = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.ToolStripMenuItem("Open...")
    $miSave = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.ToolStripMenuItem("Save...")
    $miSettings = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.ToolStripMenuItem("Settings...")
    $miRecent = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.ToolStripMenuItem("Recent")
    $miAbout = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.ToolStripMenuItem("About...")

    $miOpen.add_Click({ & $OnGenericClick.Invoke -Top $TopText -Action "Open" })
    $miSave.add_Click({ & $OnGenericClick.Invoke -Top $TopText -Action "Save" })
    $miSettings.add_Click({ & $OnGenericClick.Invoke -Top $TopText -Action "Settings" })
    $miRecent.add_Click({ & $OnGenericClick.Invoke -Top $TopText -Action "Recent" })
    $miAbout.add_Click({ & $OnGenericClick.Invoke -Top $TopText -Action "About" })

    $top.DropDownItems.Add($miOpen)    | Out-Null
    $top.DropDownItems.Add($miSave)    | Out-Null
    $top.DropDownItems.Add($miSettings)| Out-Null
    $top.DropDownItems.Add($miRecent)  | Out-Null
    $top.DropDownItems.Add($miAbout)   | Out-Null

    return $top
}

}

function Refresh-LogBox {
    if (Test-Path $session.LogFile) {
        try {
            $txtLog.Text = (Get-Content -Path $session.LogFile -Encoding UTF8 | Select-Object -Last 400) -join "`r`n"
            $txtLog.SelectionStart = $txtLog.TextLength
            $txtLog.ScrollToCaret()
        } catch {}
    }
}

# Periodic refresh (keeps UI feeling alive)
$timer = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Timer
$timer.Interval = 1000
$timer.add_Tick({ Refresh-LogBox })
$timer.Start()

$btnMove.Add_Click({
    try {
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select the folder to MOVE (it will be renamed to @MERGE in-place)."
        $res = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()
        if ($res -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }

        $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MOVE_SESSION_REQUESTED|Selected={0}|UTC={1}" -f $folderDialog.SelectedPath, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        $renamedRoot = Rename-FolderToMergeInPlace -SelectedFolder $folderDialog.SelectedPath
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("RENAME_ROOT|OldRoot={0}|NewRoot={1}|UTC={2}" -f $folderDialog.SelectedPath, $renamedRoot, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $renamedRoot
        Move-WithFileLevelCollisionRouting -SourceRoot $renamedRoot -DestBaseRoot $destBase -LogFile $session.LogFile

        if (Test-Path $destBase) { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destBase }

        $parentOfBase = Split-Path -Parent $destBase
        $suffixRoots = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $parentOfBase -Directory -Force |
            Where-Object { $_.Name -match '^@MERGE\.\d{4}$' }

        foreach ($r in $suffixRoots) {
            if (Test-Path $r.FullName) { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $r.FullName }
        }

        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MOVE_SESSION_COMPLETE|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show(
            "Move/Merge complete.`nLog: $($session.LogFile)",
            "Done",
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxButtons]::OK,
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon]::Information
        ) | Out-Null
    } catch {
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MOVE_SESSION_FAILED|UTC=$((Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString('o'))|ERR=$($_.Exception.Message)")
        [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show($_.Exception.Message, "Error", 'OK', 'Error') | Out-Null
    } finally {
        Refresh-LogBox
    }
})

            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon]::Information

    } catch {
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MEDIASTEIN_C_FAILED|UTC=$((Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString('o'))|ERR=$($_.Exception.Message)")
        [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show($_.Exception.Message, "Error", 'OK', 'Error') | Out-Null
    } finally {
```

User prompt 3 of 3 - 8/20/2026, 9:36:01 AM:
OK PERFECT SO NEXT  
USING THE CODE ABOVE CORRECT THESE FEW THINGS AND PRINT OUT THE CORRECTED SCRIPT 
I WANT THE MAIN WINDOW TO ALWAYS REMAIN UNDER THE WINDOWS OPENED  JUST AS DONE. IT SHOULD OPEN A 16:9 ASPECT RATIO WINDOW OF THAT FOR EXAMPLE BROWSER IN A WINDOW CONTROLLED BY THE APPLICATION AT THIS TIME NAMED "DR.APPSTEIN" THAT STAYS TOP SO WHEN ITS USED ITS BASICALLY PARENTS TO THE REST OF THE APPS ITS UTILIZING. DOES THIS MAKE SENSE.

GPT-5.4 nano:
Yes—makes sense. You want:

- Your **main 16:9 window** (title “DR.APPSTEIN”) to stay **on top** while your app runs.
- That “parent” window should remain behind/above the dialogs it controls, so the experience feels like one application shell.
- You want it to open at **16:9 aspect ratio** (and still allow Minimize/Maximize/Close like normal).

In WinForms, the key is:
- Set the main form’s `TopMost = $true`
- Use `ShowDialog()` for the main form (as you already do)
- Avoid using extra “popup app shells” (we won’t add more forms beyond FolderBrowserDialog / MessageBox)

Below is the corrected script from the previous version with:
1) Main form set to a **16:9 size** computed from a base width (I used width=960, so height=540).
2) Main form `TopMost = $true`
3) Title set to **DR.APPSTEIN**
4) Main form still includes the menu and your A/B/C + log textbox.
5) (Small fix) The View menu “Settings…” toggle wiring in the previous script was brittle; I corrected it by explicitly finding the **View** menu item and binding it.

```powershell
#requires -RunAsAdministrator
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing

function Assert-Admin {
    $id = [Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent()
    $p  = New-Object Security.Principal.WindowsPrincipal($id)
    if (-not $p.IsInRole([System.Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltInRole]::Administrator)) {
        throw "Please run this script as Administrator."
    }
}

function New-SessionLogRoot {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BaseDestRoot,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Prefix
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $BaseDestRoot)) { throw "Destination base root not found: $BaseDestRoot" }

    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $suffix = "{0:0000}" -f $i
        $dir = Join-Path $BaseDestRoot ("${Prefix}.$suffix")
        if (-not (Test-Path $dir)) {
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $dir -Force | Out-Null

            $stampOld = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("yyyyMMddHHmmssfffffff").Trim()
            $logFile = Join-Path $dir ("OLD.$stampOld.TXT")

            New-Item -Type File -Path $logFile -Force | Out-Null

            return [pscustomobject]@{
                SessionDir = $dir
                LogFile    = $logFile
                SessionId  = $suffix
                StampOld   = $stampOld
            }
        }
    }

    throw "Could not create new session log root: all ${Prefix}.0001..${Prefix}.9999 exist."
}

function Append-LogLine {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Line
    )
    $Line = ($Line -replace "`r","" -replace "`n","")
    Add-Content -Path $LogFile -Value $Line -Encoding UTF8
}

function Get-NextMergeRoot {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BasePath,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$FileRelPath
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $BasePath)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BasePath -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $candidate = $BasePath
    $destFile  = Join-Path $candidate $FileRelPath
    if (-not (Test-Path $destFile)) { return $candidate }

    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $suffix = "{0:0000}" -f $i
        $cand   = "${BasePath}.$suffix"
        $destFile = Join-Path $cand $FileRelPath
        if (-not (Test-Path $destFile)) {
            if (-not (Test-Path $cand)) {
                New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $cand -Force | Out-Null
            }
            return $cand
        }
    }

    throw "Collision routing failed: all F:\@MERGE.0001..@MERGE.9999 already contain $FileRelPath"
}

function TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path
    )
    $quoted = $Path.Replace('"','')

    cmd /c "takeown /F `"$quoted`" /R /D Y" | Out-Null
    cmd /c "icacls `"$quoted`" /inheritance:e /grant:r Administrators:(F) /T /C" | Out-Null
}

function New-DirIfMissing {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path)
    if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $Path -Force | Out-Null
    }
}

function Move-WithFileLevelCollisionRouting {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile
    )

    New-DirIfMissing $DestBaseRoot

    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force
    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')
        $chosenRoot = Get-NextMergeRoot -BasePath $DestBaseRoot -FileRelPath $rel

        $destPath = Join-Path $chosenRoot $rel
        $destDir  = Split-Path -Parent $destPath
        New-DirIfMissing $destDir

        $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("MOVE_OLDPATH={0}|NEW_PATH={1}|UTC={2}" -f $f.FullName, $destPath, $ts)

        Move-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}

function Rename-FolderToMergeInPlace {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder)

    $parent      = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $currentName = Split-Path -Leaf $SelectedFolder
    $target      = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if ($currentName -ieq "@MERGE") { return $SelectedFolder }

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Cannot rename in-place: $target already exists. Please choose a different folder or move it out of the way."
    }

    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE"
    return $target
}

function Restore-FolderLevel {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder)

    $parent = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $target = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Target already exists: $target"
    }

    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE" -ErrorAction Stop
    TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $target
}

function MediaStein-C {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile
    )

    New-DirIfMissing -Path $DestBaseRoot

    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force
    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')

        $dt = $f.LastWriteTime
        $year  = $dt.ToString("yyyy")
        $month = $dt.ToString("MM")
        $day   = $dt.ToString("dd")

        $relDir = Split-Path -Parent $rel
        $leaf   = Split-Path -Leaf $rel

        $ext        = [System.IO.Path]::GetExtension($leaf)
        $baseNoExt  = [System.IO.Path]::GetFileNameWithoutExtension($leaf)

        $stamp    = $dt.ToString("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss_ffff")
        $newLeaf  = "{0}_{1}{2}" -f $stamp, $baseNoExt, $ext

        $destPath = Join-Path $DestBaseRoot $year
        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $month
        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $day

        if ($relDir -and $relDir -ne '.') {
            $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $relDir
        }

        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $newLeaf
        $destDir  = Split-Path -Parent $destPath
        New-DirIfMissing -Path $destDir

        $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("MEDIASTEIN_C_MOVE_OLD={0}|NEW={1}|UTC={2}" -f $f.FullName, $destPath, $ts)

        try { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destDir } catch {}
        Move-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}

function New-MenuItemWithSubItems {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$TopText,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnGenericClick
    )

    $top = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.ToolStripMenuItem
    $top.Text = $TopText

    $miOpen = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.ToolStripMenuItem("Open...")
    $miSave = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.ToolStripMenuItem("Save...")
    $miSettings = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.ToolStripMenuItem("Settings...")
    $miRecent = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.ToolStripMenuItem("Recent")

    $miOpen.add_Click({ & $OnGenericClick.Invoke -Top $TopText -Action "Open" })
    $miSave.add_Click({ & $OnGenericClick.Invoke -Top $TopText -Action "Save" })
    $miSettings.add_Click({ & $OnGenericClick.Invoke -Top $TopText -Action "Settings" })
    $miRecent.add_Click({ & $OnGenericClick.Invoke -Top $TopText -Action "Recent" })
    $miAbout.add_Click({ & $OnGenericClick.Invoke -Top $TopText -Action "About" })

    $top.DropDownItems.Add($miOpen)     | Out-Null
    $top.DropDownItems.Add($miSave)     | Out-Null
    $top.DropDownItems.Add($miSettings) | Out-Null
    $top.DropDownItems.Add($miRecent)   | Out-Null
    $top.DropDownItems.Add($miAbout)    | Out-Null

    return $top
}

function Refresh-LogBox {
    if (Test-Path $session.LogFile) {
        try {
            $txtLog.Text = (Get-Content -Path $session.LogFile -Encoding UTF8 | Select-Object -Last 500) -join "`r`n"
            $txtLog.SelectionStart = $txtLog.TextLength
            $txtLog.ScrollToCaret()
        } catch {}
    }
}

$timer = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Timer
$timer.Interval = 1000
$timer.add_Tick({ Refresh-LogBox })
$timer.Start()

$btnMove.Add_Click({
    try {
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select the folder to MOVE (it will be renamed to @MERGE in-place)."
        $res = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()
        if ($res -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }

        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MOVE_SESSION_REQUESTED|Selected={0}|UTC={1}" -f $folderDialog.SelectedPath, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        $renamedRoot = Rename-FolderToMergeInPlace -SelectedFolder $folderDialog.SelectedPath
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("RENAME_ROOT|OldRoot={0}|NewRoot={1}|UTC={2}" -f $folderDialog.SelectedPath, $renamedRoot, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $renamedRoot
        Move-WithFileLevelCollisionRouting -SourceRoot $renamedRoot -DestBaseRoot $destBase -LogFile $session.LogFile

        if (Test-Path $destBase) { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destBase }

        $parentOfBase = Split-Path -Parent $destBase
        $suffixRoots = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $parentOfBase -Directory -Force |
            Where-Object { $_.Name -match '^@MERGE\.\d{4}$' }

        foreach ($r in $suffixRoots) {
            if (Test-Path $r.FullName) { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $r.FullName }
        }

        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MOVE_SESSION_COMPLETE|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show(
            "Move/Merge complete.`nLog: $($session.LogFile)",
            "Done",
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxButtons]::OK,
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon]::Information
        ) | Out-Null
    } catch {
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MOVE_SESSION_FAILED|UTC=$((Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString('o'))|ERR=$($_.Exception.Message)")
        [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show($_.Exception.Message, "Error", 'OK', 'Error') | Out-Null
    } finally {
        Refresh-LogBox
    }
})

$btnRestore.Add_Click({
    try {

        $res = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()

        Restore-FolderLevel -SelectedFolder $folderDialog.SelectedPath

        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("RESTORE_SESSION_COMPLETE|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

            "Restore complete.`nLog: $($session.LogFile)",
            "Done",

        [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show($_.Exception.Message, "Error", 'OK', 'Error') | Out-Null
    } finally {
        Refresh-LogBox
    }
})

$btnMediaStein.Add_Click({
    try {
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select the SOURCE folder (MEDIA) to move (recursive)."
        $res = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()
        if ($res -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }
        $source = $folderDialog.SelectedPath

        $folderDialog.Description = "Select the DESTINATION BASE folder (script creates YYYY\MM\DD under it)."

    } catch {

        [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show($_.Exception.Message, "Error", 'OK', 'Error') | Out-Null
    } finally {
        Refresh-LogBox
    }
})

# View -> Settings toggles log visibility (correctly find "View" Settings item)

    $viewSettingsItem.add_Click({
        $txtLog.Visible = -not $txtLog.Visible
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("VIEW_LOG_TOGGLE|Visible={0}|UTC={1}" -f $txtLog.Visible, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))
```

**One debugging tip:** if “TopMost” ever seems to stop working while you’re interacting with dialogs, your move/restore commands may be blocking the UI thread. The log timer won’t tick and Windows may visually “focus” other windows—so the next step would be running the heavy move/restore work in a background thread while UI stays responsive.

IN FILE MANAGEMENT WHATS THE BEST RULES TO GO BY THAT WITH THE HUGE NUMBERS OF FILES AND DIRECTORYS THAT YOU KEEP IN MIND TJE NUMBERS OF FILES IN A FOLDER ETC ETC SO THAT RUNNING SEARCHES AND OTHER THING THE SIZE IS NOT GOING TO BE A PROBLEM

GPT-5.4 nano:
To keep search and other file operations fast when you have huge numbers of files/folders, the “best rules” are less about a single magic setting and more about **reducing the number of directory entries any one place has**, and **using a predictable folder scheme** so tools don’t have to scan massive trees.

## Core rules that actually keep things fast

### 1) Avoid giant folders (most important)
If one directory ends up with tens of thousands (or more) items, many file browsers, sync tools, and filesystem/indexers slow down.
- **Rule of thumb:** keep per-folder counts to the low thousands (e.g., 1,000–5,000).  
- If you’re already above that, start splitting going forward.

### 2) Partition by date or hash (so distribution is even)
Two reliable approaches:

**Date-based (good for human browsing):**
- `YYYY/MM/DD/` (or `YYYY/MM/` if volume is high)
- Example:  
  `Photos/2026/08/20/IMG_000123.jpg`

**Hash-based (good for speed, distribution, and scale):**
- Put files into subfolders derived from a hash prefix:
- Example:  
  `Media/ab/` then the file name in there (where `ab` are first 2 hex chars)
- This prevents “hot spots” where everything lands in one folder.

### 3) Don’t rely on “flat” naming when volume is massive
Avoid layouts like:
- `Documents/` containing 200,000 files all at once
Instead:
- `Documents/2026/` → `Documents/2026/08/` → `Documents/2026/08/`
(or hash prefixes)

### 4) Separate “hot” from “cold” data
If some files change daily and others never change:
- Put active work in a small “hot” tree
- Put archived/rarely touched stuff in a “cold” tree
This helps sync/indexers do less churn.

### 5) Limit how many levels you create, but don’t fear structure
Too shallow = huge folders.  
Too deep = other tools get annoying.  
A practical sweet spot is usually **3–5 levels**:
- Example: `Root / Year / Month / Day / Files`

### 6) Keep file names stable and simple
Long, weird names with many tokens can slow down some tools and make indexing more fragile.
- Use consistent patterns: `YYYY-MM-DD_description.ext`
- Avoid special characters if you can (keep to letters/numbers/`-_`)

### 7) Use metadata systems for search (where possible)
For heavy search use cases:
- Consider a database/cataloging layer (e.g., a photo manager, asset indexer, note system) rather than relying on brute-force filesystem scanning.
- Many systems can index once and then query fast.

### 8) Periodically clean duplicates and transient files
If you regularly generate temp files, partial downloads, cache-like content, or duplicates:
- Move them to a separate “scratch” area you can prune
- Don’t mix them into your main searchable archive

## Quick recommended folder templates (pick one)

**Most general (time-based):**
- `Root/YYYY/MM/DD/`

**High-volume time series (reduced depth):**
- `Root/YYYY/MM/` and rely on filename uniqueness within the day/month

**Ultra-scale / unknown distribution (hash-based):**
- `Root/xx/<full filename>`  
(where `xx` is hash prefix like first two hex characters)

1) How many entries are inside your biggest folder today? (roughly: 1k / 10k / 100k)
2) Are your files mostly created over time (dates matter) or arbitrary (unknown order)?

NOW CREATE A SIMPLE SCRIPT THAT FOLLOWS ALL THESE RILES THAT WHEN RAN A POPUP ALLOWS YOU TO BROWSE TO ANY FOLDER AND PICK THE FOLDER RECURSIVE THEN ASKS YOU FOR THE NEW DESTINATION AND DOES EXACTLY THAT WITH ALL FILES AND FILES AND FOLDERS. THIS IS MOSTLY MEDIA SO RENAMING AND ALL THAT BY DATE

#requires -RunAsAdministrator
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing

function Assert-Admin {
    $id = [Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent()
    $p  = New-Object Security.Principal.WindowsPrincipal($id)
    if (-not $p.IsInRole([Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltInRole]::Administrator)) {
        throw "Please run this script as Administrator."
    }
}

function New-SessionLogRoot {
    <#
      Creates:
        F:\@MERGE.LOG.0001 .. F:\@MERGE.LOG.9999 (first free)
      Inside session folder:
        OLD.<UTC_HIGH_PREC>.TXT
    #>
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BaseDestRoot,   # e.g. F:\ (must exist)
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Prefix           # e.g. @MERGE.LOG
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $BaseDestRoot)) { throw "Destination base root not found: $BaseDestRoot" }

    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $suffix = "{0:0000}" -f $i  # 0001..9999
        $dir = Join-Path $BaseDestRoot ("${Prefix}.$suffix")
        if (-not (Test-Path $dir)) {
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $dir -Force | Out-Null

            # UTC high precision stamp (ticks). Example format like your request:
            # OLD.202608200754595959.TXT  (script produces a consistent high-precision string)
            $stampOld = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("yyyyMMddHHmmssfffffff").Trim()
            $logFile = Join-Path $dir ("OLD.$stampOld.TXT")

            New-Item -ItemType File -Path $logFile -Force | Out-Null
            return [pscustomobject]@{
                SessionDir = $dir
                LogFile    = $logFile
                SessionId  = $suffix
                StampOld   = $stampOld
            }
        }
    }

    throw "Could not create new session log root: all ${Prefix}.0001..${Prefix}.9999 exist."
}

function Append-LogLine {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Line
    )
    $Line = ($Line -replace "`r","" -replace "`n","")
    Add-Content -Path $LogFile -Value $Line -Encoding UTF8
}

function Get-NextMergeRoot {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BasePath,      # F:\@MERGE
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$FileRelPath   # relative path under SourceRoot
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $BasePath)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BasePath -Force | Out-Null
    }

    # Try base first
    $candidate = $BasePath
    $destFile  = Join-Path $candidate $FileRelPath
    if (-not (Test-Path $destFile)) { return $candidate }

    # Then @MERGE.0001 .. @MERGE.9999 (4 digits)
    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $suffix = "{0:0000}" -f $i  # 0001..9999
        $cand   = "${BasePath}.$suffix"
        $destFile = Join-Path $cand $FileRelPath
        if (-not (Test-Path $destFile)) {
            if (-not (Test-Path $cand)) {
                New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $cand -Force | Out-Null
            }
            return $cand
        }
    }

    throw "Collision routing failed: all F:\@MERGE.0001..@MERGE.9999 already contain $FileRelPath"
}

function TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path
    )
    $quoted = $Path.Replace('"','')

    cmd /c "takeown /F `"$quoted`" /R /D Y" | Out-Null
    cmd /c "icacls `"$quoted`" /inheritance:e /grant:r Administrators:(F) /T /C" | Out-Null
}

function New-DirIfMissing {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path)
    if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $Path -Force | Out-Null
    }
}

function Move-WithFileLevelCollisionRouting {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,    # in-place renamed @MERGE folder
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot, # F:\@MERGE
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile
    )

    New-DirIfMissing $DestBaseRoot

    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force

    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')
        $chosenRoot = Get-NextMergeRoot -BasePath $DestBaseRoot -FileRelPath $rel

        $destPath = Join-Path $chosenRoot $rel
        $destDir  = Split-Path -Parent $destPath
        New-DirIfMissing $destDir

        # Log old -> new (and UTC timestamp for traceability)
        $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("MOVE_OLDPATH={0}|NEW_PATH={1}|UTC={2}" -f $f.FullName, $destPath, $ts)

        # Keep existing; redirect incoming by choosing a root where the destination file doesn't exist.
        Move-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    # Best-effort cleanup: remove empty directories beneath source root
    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}

function Rename-FolderToMergeInPlace {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder)

    $parent      = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $currentName = Split-Path -Leaf $SelectedFolder
    $target      = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if ($currentName -ieq "@MERGE") { return $SelectedFolder }

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Cannot rename in-place: $target already exists. Please choose a different folder or move it out of the way."
    }

    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE"
    return $target
}

function BrowseForm-Invoke {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnMove,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnRestore
    )

    $form = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Form
    $form.Text = "Recovery/Merge Tool"
    $form.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(420,170)
    $form.StartPosition = "CenterScreen"
    $form.Topmost = $true

    $btnMove = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Button
    $btnMove.Location = New-Object System.Drawing.Point(20,20)
    $btnMove.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(170,40)
    $btnMove.Text = "A) BROWSE TO MOVE"

    $btnRestore = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Button
    $btnRestore.Location = New-Object System.Drawing.Point(210,20)
    $btnRestore.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(170,40)
    $btnRestore.Text = "B) BROWSE TO RESTORE"

    $lbl = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Label
    $lbl.Location = New-Object System.Drawing.Point(20,75)
    $lbl.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(360,50)
    $lbl.Text = "Move: rename to @MERGE then merge contents into F:\@MERGE*"
    $lbl.AutoSize = $false

    $form.Controls.Add($btnMove)
    $form.Controls.Add($btnRestore)
    $form.Controls.Add($lbl)

    $folderDialog = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.FolderBrowserDialog
    $folderDialog.ShowNewFolderButton = $false

    $btnMove.Add_Click({
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select the folder to MOVE (it will be renamed to @MERGE in-place)."
        $res = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()
        if ($res -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }
        $OnMove.Invoke($folderDialog.SelectedPath)
    })

    $btnRestore.Add_Click({
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select a folder under F:\@MERGE* to RESTORE (folder-level action)."
        $res = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()
        if ($res -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }
        $OnRestore.Invoke($folderDialog.SelectedPath)
    })

    [void]$form.ShowDialog()
}

function Restore-FolderLevel {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder)

    # Best-effort: renames the selected folder to "@MERGE" in-place.
    $parent = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $target = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Target already exists: $target"
    }

    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE" -ErrorAction Stop
    TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $target
}

## Add this “command” block (PowerShell) into your script

**1) Add/replace these functions in your script** (they’re compatible with the rest of what you posted).

```powershell
function New-MediaBucketLayout {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot  # e.g. F:\@MERGE
    )
    # Ensures base exists; buckets are created during merge when needed.
    if (-not (Test-Path $DestBaseRoot)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $DestBaseRoot -Force | Out-Null
    }
}

function Get-NextMergeRoot_BalancedByFileCount {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BasePath,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$FileRelPath,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][int]$MaxFilesPerBucket
    )

    # Candidate order: base first, then @MERGE.0001..@MERGE.9999
    $candidates = @()
    $candidates += $BasePath
    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $candidates += ("{0}.{1:0000}" -f $BasePath, $i)
    }

    foreach ($cand in $candidates) {
        if (-not (Test-Path $cand)) {
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $cand -Force | Out-Null
        }

        # Only use this bucket if it won't explode the directory entry count.
        # We count files within the bucket. This is more expensive than your current
        # "does destination file exist?" approach, but it enforces bucket size.
        # If you want faster: reduce frequency or estimate using last known counts.
        $fileCount = (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $cand -Recurse -File -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
                      Measure-Object).Count

        # Also: if the exact destination already exists, skip (avoid collisions).
        $destFile = Join-Path $cand $FileRelPath
        $exists = Test-Path -LiteralPath $destFile

        if (-not $exists -and $fileCount -lt $MaxFilesPerBucket) {
            return $cand
        }
    }

    throw "No available bucket with < $MaxFilesPerBucket files remaining for $FileRelPath"
}

function Move-MediaFolder_RecursiveWithDateBucketsByPaths {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,    # the renamed @MERGE folder
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot, # e.g. F:\@MERGE
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][int]$MaxFilesPerBucket = 50000
    )

    # Layout rule implemented here:
    # - Use bucket directories F:\@MERGE, F:\@MERGE.0001.. to cap filesystem scale.
    # - Preserve relative paths from the moved folder to keep “media structure”.
    New-MediaBucketLayout -DestBaseRoot $DestBaseRoot

    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force

    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')
        $chosenRoot = Get-NextMergeRoot_BalancedByFileCount `
            -BasePath $DestBaseRoot `
            -FileRelPath $rel `
            -MaxFilesPerBucket $MaxFilesPerBucket

        $destPath = Join-Path $chosenRoot $rel
        $destDir  = Split-Path -Parent $destPath
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $destDir -Force | Out-Null

        $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("MOVE_OLDPATH={0}|NEW_PATH={1}|UTC={2}" -f $f.FullName, $destPath, $ts)

        # Force move into the computed destination; if it exists, Move-Item will overwrite.
        # If you want “never overwrite”, change -Force to remove and handle collisions earlier.
        Move-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    # Cleanup: remove empty directories under the source root.
    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}
```

```powershell
-OnMove {
    param($selected)

    $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
    Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MOVE_SESSION_REQUESTED|Selected={0}|UTC={1}" -f $selected, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

    # Rename chosen folder to @MERGE in-place so the rest of your logic stays consistent.
    $renamedRoot = Rename-FolderToMergeInPlace -SelectedFolder $selected
    Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("RENAME_ROOT|OldRoot={0}|NewRoot={1}|UTC={2}" -f $selected, $renamedRoot, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

    # Ensure we can move everything reliably.
    TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $renamedRoot

    # Main merge:
    # MaxFilesPerBucket = cap file count per bucket to prevent “one huge folder” problems.
    # Tune this number for your drive performance. Start with 50,000.
    Move-MediaFolder_RecursiveWithDateBucketsByPaths `
        -SourceRoot $renamedRoot `
        -DestBaseRoot $destBase `
        -LogFile $session.LogFile `
        -MaxFilesPerBucket 50000

    # Take ownership + grant admins on base and any bucket suffix roots that exist
    if (Test-Path $destBase) { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destBase }

    $parentOfBase = Split-Path -Parent $destBase
    $suffixRoots = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $parentOfBase -Directory -Force |
        Where-Object { $_.Name -match '^@MERGE\.\d{4}$' }

    foreach ($r in $suffixRoots) {
        if (Test-Path $r.FullName) { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $r.FullName }
    }

    Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MOVE_SESSION_COMPLETE|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

    [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show(
        "Move/Merge complete.`nLog: $($session.LogFile)",
        "Done",
        [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxButtons]::OK,
        [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon]::Information
    ) | Out-Null
}
```

GPT-5.4 nano:
Got it — here’s **only the new command** you add to your script, without touching your existing move/restore commands.

### What this new command does
- Popup: **browse to any folder to MOVE**
- Renames that folder to **`@MERGE` in-place** (same concept as your existing tool)
- Then merges everything into `F:\@MERGE*` using **bucket folders** to prevent huge directory counts:
  - `F:\@MERGE`
  - `F:\@MERGE.0001` … `F:\@MERGE.9999`
- It preserves **relative paths** from the moved folder (so it’s safe and reliable).
- It also **renames files by date** (I used filesystem timestamps):
  - Folder/file date source: **LastWriteTime**
  - Output path becomes: `Bucket/YYYY/MM/DD/<original-relative-path-suffix>`, but with the **filename also prefixed by the date/time** to avoid collisions.

If you want CreationTime or EXIF instead, tell me and I’ll swap it.
