## 1) Paste these NEW functions (add them anywhere before MAIN)

```powershell
function Get-DateParts-FromFile {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][System.IO.FileInfo]$File
    )

    # Date source: LastWriteTime (filesystem)
    $dt = $File.LastWriteTime

    return [pscustomobject]@{
        Year  = $dt.ToString("yyyy")
        Month = $dt.ToString("MM")
        Day   = $dt.ToString("dd")
        Stamp = $dt.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd_HH-mm-ss-ffff")
    }
}

function Get-NextMergeRoot_ByBucketCapacity {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BasePath,     # e.g. F:\@MERGE
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$FileRelPath,  # relative path from moved root (used only for collision path)
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][int]$MaxFilesPerBucket
    )

    # Candidate order: base first, then suffix buckets
    $candidates = @()
    $candidates += $BasePath
    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $candidates += ("{0}.{1:0000}" -f $BasePath, $i)
    }

    foreach ($cand in $candidates) {
        if (-not (Test-Path $cand)) {
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $cand -Force | Out-Null
        }

        # Keep directory entry counts from exploding by capping file count in the bucket.
        # (This is slower than your original existence-only routing, but enforces the rule.)
        $fileCount = (Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $cand -Recurse -File -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
                      Measure-Object).Count

        if ($fileCount -lt $MaxFilesPerBucket) {
            $destFile = Join-Path $cand $FileRelPath
            if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $destFile)) {
                return $cand
            }
        }
    }

    throw "No bucket capacity available in $BasePath..$($BasePath).0001..9999 with MaxFilesPerBucket=$MaxFilesPerBucket"
}

function Move-MediaFolder_NewCommand_DateBuckets {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,    # renamed @MERGE folder
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot, # F:\@MERGE
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][int]$MaxFilesPerBucket = 50000
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $DestBaseRoot)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $DestBaseRoot -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force

    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $fi = $f
        $rel = $fi.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')

        $parts = Get-DateParts-FromFile -File $fi

        # Preserve the "folder structure part" but we will rebuild filename with date/time prefix
        $relDir  = Split-Path -Parent $rel
        $leaf    = Split-Path -Leaf $rel
        $baseName = [System.IO.Path]::GetFileNameWithoutExtension($leaf)
        $ext      = [System.IO.Path]::GetExtension($leaf)

        # Final file name includes datetime to reduce collisions when multiple files share a date
        $newLeaf = "{0}_{1}{2}" -f $parts.Stamp, $baseName, $ext

        # Build relative destination path under bucket:
        # YYYY/MM/DD/<original-relative-dir>/newLeaf
        $relDest = Join-Path $parts.Year (Join-Path $parts.Month (Join-Path $parts.Day (Join-Path ($relDir) $newLeaf)))

        $chosenRoot = Get-NextMergeRoot_ByBucketCapacity -BasePath $DestBaseRoot -FileRelPath $relDest -MaxFilesPerBucket $MaxFilesPerBucket

        $destPath = Join-Path $chosenRoot $relDest
        $destDir  = Split-Path -Parent $destPath
        if (-not (Test-Path $destDir)) {
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $destDir -Force | Out-Null
        }

        $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("MOVE2_OLDPATH={0}|NEW_PATH={1}|UTC={2}" -f $fi.FullName, $destPath, $ts)

        Move-Item -LiteralPath $fi.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    # Best-effort: remove empty directories in source
    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}
```

## 1) Add this NEW function: `MediaStein-C`

Paste anywhere before `# ---------------- MAIN ----------------`:

```powershell
function MediaStein-C {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot
    )

    # Ensure destination base exists
    New-DirIfMissing -Path $DestBaseRoot

    # Move all files AND folders "by date":
    # We preserve relative paths, but rebuild the destination root as:
    # DestBaseRoot\YYYY\MM\DD\<original relative path>
    # For collisions, we prefix the filename with timestamp.
    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force

    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $fi = $f
        $rel = $fi.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')

        # Date source (filesystem): LastWriteTime
        $dt = $fi.LastWriteTime
        $year  = $dt.ToString("yyyy")
        $month = $dt.ToString("MM")
        $day   = $dt.ToString("dd")

        $relDir  = Split-Path -Parent $rel
        $leaf    = Split-Path -Leaf $rel
        $ext     = [System.IO.Path]::GetExtension($leaf)
        $baseNoExt = [System.IO.Path]::GetFileNameWithoutExtension($leaf)

        # Timestamp prefix to reduce collisions for same-date uploads
        $stamp = $dt.ToString("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss_ffff")
        $newLeaf = "{0}_{1}{2}" -f $stamp, $baseNoExt, $ext

        $destPath = Join-Path $DestBaseRoot $year
        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $month
        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $day
        if ($relDir -and $relDir -ne '.') {
            $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $relDir
        }
        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $newLeaf

        $destDir = Split-Path -Parent $destPath
        New-DirIfMissing -Path $destDir

        # Best-effort log
        try {
            $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
            Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MEDIASTEIN_C_MOVE_OLD={0}|NEW={1}|UTC={2}" -f $fi.FullName, $destPath, $ts)
        } catch {}

        # Take ownership of destination directory (optional but improves reliability)
        try { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destDir } catch {}

        # Move file (force)
        Move-Item -LiteralPath $fi.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    # Cleanup: attempt to remove empty directories under source root
    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}
```

### Important
- This function is **only about the move**. It does **not** rename the source folder to `@MERGE`.
- It **rebuilds by date** on the way into the destination.

If you want it to also do the “rename source folder to @MERGE first” behavior, say so and I’ll adjust.

```powershell
BrowseForm-Invoke `
    -OnMove {
        # (your existing A block unchanged)
        param($selected)

        $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MOVE_SESSION_REQUESTED|Selected={0}|UTC={1}" -f $selected, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $renamedRoot

        Move-WithFileLevelCollisionRouting -SourceRoot $renamedRoot -DestBaseRoot $destBase -LogFile $session.LogFile

        if (Test-Path $destBase) { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destBase }

        $parentOfBase = Split-Path -Parent $destBase
        $suffixRoots = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $parentOfBase -Directory -Force |
            Where-Object { $_.Name -match '^@MERGE\.\d{4}$' }

        foreach ($r in $suffixRoots) {
            if (Test-Path $r.FullName) { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $r.FullName }
        }

        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MOVE_SESSION_COMPLETE|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        param($sourceRoot, $destBaseRoot)

        $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MEDIASTEIN_C_SESSION_REQUESTED|Source={0}|Dest={1}|UTC={2}" -f $sourceRoot, $destBaseRoot, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        # Optional: ensure ownership for source and destination
        try { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $sourceRoot } catch {}
        try { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destBaseRoot } catch {}

        # Run the new command
```

#requires -RunAsAdministrator
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing

function Assert-Admin {
    $id = [Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent()
    $p  = New-Object Security.Principal.WindowsPrincipal($id)
    if (-not $p.IsInRole([Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltInRole]::Administrator)) {
        throw "Please run this script as Administrator."
    }
}

function New-SessionLogRoot {
    <#
      Creates:
        F:\@MERGE.LOG.0001 .. F:\@MERGE.LOG.9999 (first free)
      Inside session folder:
        OLD.<UTC_HIGH_PREC>.TXT
    #>
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BaseDestRoot,   # e.g. F:\ (must exist)
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Prefix           # e.g. @MERGE.LOG
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $BaseDestRoot)) { throw "Destination base root not found: $BaseDestRoot" }

    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $suffix = "{0:0000}" -f $i  # 0001..9999
        $dir = Join-Path $BaseDestRoot ("${Prefix}.$suffix")
        if (-not (Test-Path $dir)) {
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $dir -Force | Out-Null

            # UTC high precision stamp (ticks). Example format like your request:
            # OLD.202608200754595959.TXT  (script produces a consistent high-precision string)
            $stampOld = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("yyyyMMddHHmmssfffffff").Trim()
            $logFile = Join-Path $dir ("OLD.$stampOld.TXT")

            New-Item -ItemType File -Path $logFile -Force | Out-Null
            return [pscustomobject]@{
                SessionDir = $dir
                LogFile    = $logFile
                SessionId  = $suffix
                StampOld   = $stampOld
            }
        }
    }

    throw "Could not create new session log root: all ${Prefix}.0001..${Prefix}.9999 exist."
}

function Append-LogLine {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Line
    )
    $Line = ($Line -replace "`r","" -replace "`n","")
    Add-Content -Path $LogFile -Value $Line -Encoding UTF8
}

function Get-NextMergeRoot {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BasePath,      # F:\@MERGE
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$FileRelPath   # relative path under SourceRoot
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $BasePath)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BasePath -Force | Out-Null
    }

    # Try base first
    $candidate = $BasePath
    $destFile  = Join-Path $candidate $FileRelPath
    if (-not (Test-Path $destFile)) { return $candidate }

    # Then @MERGE.0001 .. @MERGE.9999 (4 digits)
    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $suffix = "{0:0000}" -f $i  # 0001..9999
        $cand   = "${BasePath}.$suffix"
        $destFile = Join-Path $cand $FileRelPath
        if (-not (Test-Path $destFile)) {
            if (-not (Test-Path $cand)) {
                New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $cand -Force | Out-Null
            }
            return $cand
        }
    }

    throw "Collision routing failed: all F:\@MERGE.0001..@MERGE.9999 already contain $FileRelPath"
}

function TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path
    )
    $quoted = $Path.Replace('"','')

    cmd /c "takeown /F `"$quoted`" /R /D Y" | Out-Null
    cmd /c "icacls `"$quoted`" /inheritance:e /grant:r Administrators:(F) /T /C" | Out-Null
}

function New-DirIfMissing {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path)
    if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $Path -Force | Out-Null
    }
}

function Move-WithFileLevelCollisionRouting {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,    # in-place renamed @MERGE folder
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot, # F:\@MERGE
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile
    )

    New-DirIfMissing $DestBaseRoot

    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force

    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')
        $chosenRoot = Get-NextMergeRoot -BasePath $DestBaseRoot -FileRelPath $rel

        $destPath = Join-Path $chosenRoot $rel
        $destDir  = Split-Path -Parent $destPath
        New-DirIfMissing $destDir

        # Log old -> new (and UTC timestamp for traceability)
        $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("MOVE_OLDPATH={0}|NEW_PATH={1}|UTC={2}" -f $f.FullName, $destPath, $ts)

        # Keep existing; redirect incoming by choosing a root where the destination file doesn't exist.
        Move-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    # Best-effort cleanup: remove empty directories beneath source root
    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}

function Rename-FolderToMergeInPlace {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder)

    $parent      = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $currentName = Split-Path -Leaf $SelectedFolder
    $target      = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if ($currentName -ieq "@MERGE") { return $SelectedFolder }

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Cannot rename in-place: $target already exists. Please choose a different folder or move it out of the way."
    }

    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE"
    return $target
}

function BrowseForm-Invoke {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnMove,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnRestore
    )

    $form = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Form
    $form.Text = "Recovery/Merge Tool"
    $form.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(420,170)
    $form.StartPosition = "CenterScreen"
    $form.Topmost = $true

    $btnMove = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Button
    $btnMove.Location = New-Object System.Drawing.Point(20,20)
    $btnMove.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(170,40)
    $btnMove.Text = "A) BROWSE TO MOVE"

    $btnRestore = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Button
    $btnRestore.Location = New-Object System.Drawing.Point(210,20)
    $btnRestore.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(170,40)
    $btnRestore.Text = "B) BROWSE TO RESTORE"

    $lbl = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Label
    $lbl.Location = New-Object System.Drawing.Point(20,75)
    $lbl.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(360,50)
    $lbl.Text = "Move: rename to @MERGE then merge contents into F:\@MERGE*"
    $lbl.AutoSize = $false

    $form.Controls.Add($btnMove)
    $form.Controls.Add($btnRestore)
    $form.Controls.Add($lbl)

    $folderDialog = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.FolderBrowserDialog
    $folderDialog.ShowNewFolderButton = $false

    $btnMove.Add_Click({
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select the folder to MOVE (it will be renamed to @MERGE in-place)."
        $res = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()
        if ($res -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }
        $OnMove.Invoke($folderDialog.SelectedPath)
    })

    $btnRestore.Add_Click({
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select a folder under F:\@MERGE* to RESTORE (folder-level action)."
        $res = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()
        if ($res -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }
        $OnRestore.Invoke($folderDialog.SelectedPath)
    })

    [void]$form.ShowDialog()
}

function Restore-FolderLevel {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder)

    # Best-effort: renames the selected folder to "@MERGE" in-place.
    $parent = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $target = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Target already exists: $target"
    }

    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE" -ErrorAction Stop
    TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $target
}
function MediaStein-C {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot
    )

    # Ensure destination base exists
    New-DirIfMissing -Path $DestBaseRoot

    # Move all files AND folders "by date":
    # We preserve relative paths, but rebuild the destination root as:
    # DestBaseRoot\YYYY\MM\DD\<original relative path>
    # For collisions, we prefix the filename with timestamp.
    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force

    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $fi = $f
        $rel = $fi.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')

        # Date source (filesystem): LastWriteTime
        $dt = $fi.LastWriteTime
        $year  = $dt.ToString("yyyy")
        $month = $dt.ToString("MM")
        $day   = $dt.ToString("dd")

        $relDir  = Split-Path -Parent $rel
        $leaf    = Split-Path -Leaf $rel
        $ext     = [System.IO.Path]::GetExtension($leaf)
        $baseNoExt = [System.IO.Path]::GetFileNameWithoutExtension($leaf)

        # Timestamp prefix to reduce collisions for same-date uploads
        $stamp = $dt.ToString("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss_ffff")
        $newLeaf = "{0}_{1}{2}" -f $stamp, $baseNoExt, $ext

        $destPath = Join-Path $DestBaseRoot $year
        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $month
        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $day
        if ($relDir -and $relDir -ne '.') {
            $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $relDir
        }
        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $newLeaf

        $destDir = Split-Path -Parent $destPath
        New-DirIfMissing -Path $destDir

        # Best-effort log
        try {
            $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
            Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MEDIASTEIN_C_MOVE_OLD={0}|NEW={1}|UTC={2}" -f $fi.FullName, $destPath, $ts)
        } catch {}

        # Take ownership of destination directory (optional but improves reliability)
        try { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destDir } catch {}

        # Move file (force)
        Move-Item -LiteralPath $fi.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    # Cleanup: attempt to remove empty directories under source root
    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}

$destBase = "F:\@MERGE"
$session = New-SessionLogRoot -BaseDestRoot "F:\" -Prefix "@MERGE.LOG"

Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("SESSION_START|SessionDir={0}|LogFile={1}|UTC={2}" -f $session.SessionDir, $session.LogFile, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))
Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("SESSION_INPUT|StartUser={0}|Computer={1}|UTC={2}" -f $env:USERNAME, $env:COMPUTERNAME, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

BrowseForm-Invoke `
    -OnMove {
        param($selected)

        $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MOVE_SESSION_REQUESTED|Selected={0}|UTC={1}" -f $selected, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        $renamedRoot = Rename-FolderToMergeInPlace -SelectedFolder $selected
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("RENAME_ROOT|OldRoot={0}|NewRoot={1}|UTC={2}" -f $selected, $renamedRoot, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        # Take ownership + grant admins before heavy operations (more reliable)
        TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $renamedRoot

        # Move with file-level collision routing + log per file
        Move-WithFileLevelCollisionRouting -SourceRoot $renamedRoot -DestBaseRoot $destBase -LogFile $session.LogFile

        # Take ownership + grant admins on base and any suffix roots that exist
        if (Test-Path $destBase) { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destBase }

        $parentOfBase = Split-Path -Parent $destBase
        $suffixRoots = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $parentOfBase -Directory -Force |
            Where-Object { $_.Name -match '^@MERGE\.\d{4}$' }

        foreach ($r in $suffixRoots) {
            if (Test-Path $r.FullName) { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $r.FullName }
        }

        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MOVE_SESSION_COMPLETE|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show(
            "Move/Merge complete.`nLog: $($session.LogFile)",
            "Done",
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxButtons]::OK,
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon]::Information
        ) | Out-Null
    } `
    -OnRestore {
        param($selectedRestoreFolder)

        $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("RESTORE_SESSION_REQUESTED|Selected={0}|UTC={1}" -f $selectedRestoreFolder, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        Restore-FolderLevel -SelectedFolder $selectedRestoreFolder

        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("RESTORE_SESSION_COMPLETE|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon]::Information
        ) | Out-Null

```powershell
#requires -RunAsAdministrator
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing

function Assert-Admin {
    $id = [Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent()
    $p  = New-Object Security.Principal.WindowsPrincipal($id)
    if (-not $p.IsInRole([System.Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltInRole]::Administrator)) {
        throw "Please run this script as Administrator."
    }
}

function New-SessionLogRoot {
    <#
      Creates:
        F:\@MERGE.LOG.0001 .. F:\@MERGE.LOG.9999 (first free)
      Inside session folder:
        OLD.<UTC_HIGH_PREC>.TXT
    #>
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BaseDestRoot,   # e.g. F:\ (must exist)
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Prefix           # e.g. @MERGE.LOG
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $BaseDestRoot)) { throw "Destination base root not found: $BaseDestRoot" }

    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $suffix = "{0:0000}" -f $i  # 0001..9999
        $dir = Join-Path $BaseDestRoot ("${Prefix}.$suffix")
        if (-not (Test-Path $dir)) {
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $dir -Force | Out-Null

            $stampOld = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("yyyyMMddHHmmssfffffff").Trim()
            $logFile = Join-Path $dir ("OLD.$stampOld.TXT")

            New-Item -ItemType File -Path $logFile -Force | Out-Null
            return [pscustomobject]@{
                SessionDir = $dir
                LogFile    = $logFile
                SessionId  = $suffix
                StampOld   = $stampOld
            }
        }
    }

    throw "Could not create new session log root: all ${Prefix}.0001..${Prefix}.9999 exist."
}

function Append-LogLine {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Line
    )
    $Line = ($Line -replace "`r","" -replace "`n","")
    Add-Content -Path $LogFile -Value $Line -Encoding UTF8
}

function Get-NextMergeRoot {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BasePath,      # F:\@MERGE
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$FileRelPath   # relative path under SourceRoot
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $BasePath)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BasePath -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $candidate = $BasePath
    $destFile  = Join-Path $candidate $FileRelPath
    if (-not (Test-Path $destFile)) { return $candidate }

    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $suffix = "{0:0000}" -f $i  # 0001..9999
        $cand   = "${BasePath}.$suffix"
        $destFile = Join-Path $cand $FileRelPath
        if (-not (Test-Path $destFile)) {
            if (-not (Test-Path $cand)) {
                New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $cand -Force | Out-Null
            }
            return $cand
        }
    }

    throw "Collision routing failed: all F:\@MERGE.0001..@MERGE.9999 already contain $FileRelPath"
}

function TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path
    )
    $quoted = $Path.Replace('"','')

    cmd /c "takeown /F `"$quoted`" /R /D Y" | Out-Null
    cmd /c "icacls `"$quoted`" /inheritance:e /grant:r Administrators:(F) /T /C" | Out-Null
}

function New-DirIfMissing {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path)
    if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $Path -Force | Out-Null
    }
}

function Move-WithFileLevelCollisionRouting {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,    # in-place renamed @MERGE folder
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot, # F:\@MERGE
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile
    )

    New-DirIfMissing $DestBaseRoot

    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force

    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')
        $chosenRoot = Get-NextMergeRoot -BasePath $DestBaseRoot -FileRelPath $rel

        $destPath = Join-Path $chosenRoot $rel
        $destDir  = Split-Path -Parent $destPath
        New-DirIfMissing $destDir

        $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("MOVE_OLDPATH={0}|NEW_PATH={1}|UTC={2}" -f $f.FullName, $destPath, $ts)

        Move-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}

function Rename-FolderToMergeInPlace {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder)

    $parent      = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $currentName = Split-Path -Leaf $SelectedFolder
    $target      = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if ($currentName -ieq "@MERGE") { return $SelectedFolder }

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Cannot rename in-place: $target already exists. Please choose a different folder or move it out of the way."
    }

    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE"
    return $target
}

function Restore-FolderLevel {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder)

    $parent = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $target = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Target already exists: $target"
    }

    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE" -ErrorAction Stop
    TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $target
}

# =========================
# NEW: C) MEDIASTEIN
# =========================
function MediaStein-C {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,    # folder to move (picked in popup)
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot, # destination base folder (picked in popup)
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile
    )

    # Date + size control via filesystem date buckets.
    # Output: DestBaseRoot\YYYY\MM\DD\<original relative dir>\YYYYMMDD_HHMMSS_ffff_<originalname>.<ext>
    New-DirIfMissing -Path $DestBaseRoot

    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force

    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')

        $dt = $f.LastWriteTime  # "by date" (filesystem LastWriteTime)
        $year  = $dt.ToString("yyyy")
        $month = $dt.ToString("MM")
        $day   = $dt.ToString("dd")

        $relDir = Split-Path -Parent $rel
        $leaf   = Split-Path -Leaf $rel

        $ext        = [System.IO.Path]::GetExtension($leaf)
        $baseNoExt  = [System.IO.Path]::GetFileNameWithoutExtension($leaf)

        $stamp    = $dt.ToString("yyyyMMdd_HHmmss_ffff")
        $newLeaf  = "{0}_{1}{2}" -f $stamp, $baseNoExt, $ext

        $destPath = Join-Path $DestBaseRoot $year
        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $month
        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $day

        if ($relDir -and $relDir -ne '.') {
            $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $relDir
        }

        $destPath = Join-Path $destPath $newLeaf
        $destDir  = Split-Path -Parent $destPath
        New-DirIfMissing -Path $destDir

        $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("MEDIASTEIN_C_MOVE_OLD={0}|NEW={1}|UTC={2}" -f $f.FullName, $destPath, $ts)

        try { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destDir } catch {}

        # Force move if needed
        Move-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    # Cleanup empty directories under the source root
    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}

    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnMove,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnRestore,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnMediaSteinC
    )

    $form = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Form
    $form.Text = "Recovery/Merge Tool"
    $form.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(420,210)
    $form.StartPosition = "CenterScreen"
    $form.Topmost = $true

    $btnMove = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Button

    $btnRestore = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Button

    $btnRestore.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(170,40)
    $btnRestore.Text = "B) BROWSE TO RESTORE"

    $btnMediaStein = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Button
    $btnMediaStein.Location = New-Object System.Drawing.Point(20,120)
    $btnMediaStein.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(370,35)
    $btnMediaStein.Text = "C) MEDIASTEIN (Move by date into your chosen destination)"

    $lbl = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Label
    $lbl.Location = New-Object System.Drawing.Point(20,75)
    $lbl.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(380,40)
    $lbl.Text = "A: rename to @MERGE then merge into F:\@MERGE*  |  B: folder-level restore"
    $lbl.AutoSize = $false

    $form.Controls.Add($btnMove)
    $form.Controls.Add($btnRestore)
    $form.Controls.Add($btnMediaStein)
    $form.Controls.Add($lbl)

    $folderDialog = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.FolderBrowserDialog
    $folderDialog.ShowNewFolderButton = $false

    $btnMove.Add_Click({
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select the folder to MOVE (it will be renamed to @MERGE in-place)."
        $res = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()
        if ($res -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }
        $OnMove.Invoke($folderDialog.SelectedPath)
    })

    $btnRestore.Add_Click({
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select a folder under F:\@MERGE* to RESTORE (folder-level action)."
        $res = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()
        if ($res -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }
        $OnRestore.Invoke($folderDialog.SelectedPath)
    })

    $btnMediaStein.Add_Click({
        # Pick SOURCE folder
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select the SOURCE folder (MEDIA) to move (recursive)."
        $res = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()
        if ($res -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }
        $source = $folderDialog.SelectedPath

        # Pick DESTINATION BASE folder (THIS fixes your A/B issue: not auto-picking F:\)
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select the DESTINATION BASE folder (script creates YYYY\\MM\\DD under it)."
        $res2 = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()
        if ($res2 -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }
        $dest = $folderDialog.SelectedPath

        $OnMediaSteinC.Invoke($source, $dest)
    })

    [void]$form.ShowDialog()
}

        if (Test-Path $destBase) { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destBase }

        $parentOfBase = Split-Path -Parent $destBase
        $suffixRoots = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $parentOfBase -Directory -Force |

        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MOVE_SESSION_COMPLETE|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

    -OnRestore {
        param($selectedRestoreFolder)

        $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

        Restore-FolderLevel -SelectedFolder $selectedRestoreFolder

        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("RESTORE_SESSION_COMPLETE|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show(
            "Restore complete.`nLog: $($session.LogFile)",
            "Done",
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxButtons]::OK,
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon]::Information
        ) | Out-Null
    } `
    -OnMediaSteinC {
        param($sourceRoot, $destBaseRoot)

        $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MEDIASTEIN_C_SESSION_REQUESTED|Source={0}|DestBase={1}|UTC={2}" -f $sourceRoot, $destBaseRoot, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        # best-effort to make destination accessible
        try { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destBaseRoot } catch {}
        try { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $sourceRoot } catch {}

        MediaStein-C -SourceRoot $sourceRoot -DestBaseRoot $destBaseRoot -LogFile $session.LogFile

        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MEDIASTEIN_C_SESSION_COMPLETE|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show(
            "MEDIASTEIN complete (date move).`nLog: $($session.LogFile)",
            "Done",
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxButtons]::OK,
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon]::Information
        ) | Out-Null
    }
```

GPT-5.4 nano:
Got it. I’ll be specific and also explain what I need and why.

## 1) “777” on Windows 11: what does it mean and what differences are there?
On Windows NTFS, permissions are controlled by **ACLs** (Access Control Lists) and **ownership**—not a numeric “mode” like Linux.

So there are two different “intents” people often mean by “777”:

### Option A — Linux-style intent (“world-writable”)
- Goal: “Everyone can read/write/execute.”
- Windows equivalent: grant **Full Control** broadly (commonly to **Everyone**, and/or **Users**) and ensure it applies **recursively**.

### Option B — Admin/recovery intent (“you (admin) can take over and fully manage it”)
- Goal: “Even if files are owned by SYSTEM/TrustedInstaller/another SID, admin can recover them.”
- Windows equivalent: set **ownership to Administrators** (or the admin user), then grant **Full Control** to Administrators (and possibly your admin account).

### Key differences
- **Ownership** controls who is allowed to change permissions.
- **ACLs** determine who can read/write/delete.
- If you only change ACLs but don’t take ownership first, many “Access denied” failures occur during recovery.
- If you take ownership but grant ACLs too narrowly, you may still not be able to modify/delete everything.

**What I need from you:** which intent you want:
- “Full Control for Everyone everywhere” (closest to “777”), or
- “Full Control for Administrators only” (often safer and more reliable during recovery)

Tell me which one you prefer.

---

## 2) Clarifying the popup folder operation (what I need)
You described:

- “POPUP MENU”
  - **A. BROWSE TO MOVE**
    - “RENAMES THE FOLDER PICKED ‘@MERGE’ & MOVES THE FOLDER @MERGE RECURSIVE TO F:\”
  - **B. BROWSE TO RESTORE**
    - “RENAMES THE FOLDER PICKED ‘@MERGE’ & MOVES THE FOLDER @MERGE RECURSIVE TO F:\ & RESTORES THE FILENAME AND OR EXTENSION”

The missing detail is: *what exact source/target relationship you want* when you pick something in each menu action.

### For A) “Browse to Move”
When you browse and select a folder, there are two common workflows:

#### Workflow A1 (rename-then-move)
1. You select `C:\some\folder`
2. Script renames it to `@MERGE` (so it becomes effectively `C:\some\@MERGE`)
3. Script then moves it recursively to `F:\` as `F:\@MERGE`

Result: the original folder name is lost (but the contents are moved).

#### Workflow A2 (move-then-rename)
1. You select `C:\some\folder`
2. Script moves the folder to `F:\`

## 4) Clarifying restoring original filename/extension (this is the biggest constraint)
You asked:

> “CAN A FILE THAT HAS BEEN RENAMED BE RECOVERED BACK TO ITS ORIGINAL NAME AND EXTENSION?  
> IF SO… BROWSE TO RESTORE… RESTORES THE FILENAME AND OR EXTENSION…”

Here’s the truth in Windows terms:

- A file’s **original name/extension is not stored in the file contents** in a way Windows can automatically revert later.
- If renaming happened, Windows only knows the *current* name and extension.
- To restore the *original* names/extensions, you need either:
  1) a **record/mapping** from original → renamed, or
  2) a **reversible naming rule** that you can reverse mechanically.

### Examples of “reversible rule” (works)
- If your earlier process renamed like:
  - `report.pdf` → `report.pdf__MERGED` (extension changed in a known way)
  - then restoring is deterministic.
- Or:
  - `report.pdf` → `report.__pdf` (rule is reversible)
- Or:
  - moved files but kept the original in another variable (like copying to a manifest)

### Examples of “not reversible without a mapping” (does NOT work automatically)
- If the earlier process simply renamed everything to a generic name (e.g., `file1`, `file2`, or moved into `@MERGE` and lost original names) with no log:
  - there’s nothing to compute the original extension/name from.
  - you can only rename back *to something you stored*.

### What I need from you
Which of these is true for your situation?

- **Case 1:** You have a log/manifest (CSV/TXT/JSON) mapping renamed → original.
- **Case 2:** The rename follows a consistent rule (tell me the rule you used previously).
- **Case 3:** No log and no clear rule (then “restore original names/extensions” cannot be done; we can only recover folder structure/permissions and maybe remove the `@MERGE` name, not reconstruct original filenames).

Also, do you want B) to restore:
- only **the folder name** (e.g., remove/undo `@MERGE`), or
- also **each file’s original name/extension** inside?

---

User prompt 3 of 16 - 8/20/2026, 6:57:05 AM:
ON 1 WHICH WILL GENERALLY BE BEST SHOULD I NEED TO MANIPULATE IN LINUX AND WINDOWS 

GPT-5.4 nano:
For recovery scenarios on **Windows 11**, the closest “generally best” equivalent to Linux “777” is:

- **Administrators (and the selected admin user) full control**, plus
- enough inheritance/ACL propagation so you don’t get “Access denied” during recursive operations.

So in your terms: choose **(B) Full Control for Administrators only** as the **default**.

### Why Administrators-only is generally best on Windows
- Windows recovery failures are more often caused by **ownership + ACL restrictions** than by read permissions alone.
- Granting Full Control to **everyone** can *work*, but it’s riskier and sometimes slower/less reliable in enterprise/security contexts (and it changes access broadly).
- Administrators-only focuses on the recovery goal: **take over and regain control**, without re-widening access for all users.

### Practical recommendation
Use:
- **Take ownership recursively** (or force ownership to Administrators)
- **Grant Full Control recursively to Administrators**
- Optionally also grant Full Control to your current admin account explicitly (some environments behave better that way)

### When “Everyone = Full Control” is justified
Only if you truly need every local user (and potentially services running under other SIDs) to access/modify everything after recovery.

1) **Administrators-only** (recommended), or
2) **Everyone** (world-writable “777-equivalent”)

GPT-5.4 nano:
Yes—this is the right way to think about it. Here’s the reasoning and then I’ll give you the exact collision policy options the script can implement.

## What “file collision” means (in your terms)
A collision occurs when, during the recursive move, the script is about to write an incoming file to a destination location where **a file already exists at the exact same destination path** (same relative folder path + same filename + same extension).

Concretely:
- Incoming: `@MERGE\subdir\abc.dll`
- Destination (example): `F:\@MERGE.0003\subdir\abc.dll`
- If `F:\@MERGE.0003\subdir\abc.dll` already exists, that’s a collision.

## Why you need a collision policy
If the destination already has that exact file path, you have 2 fundamentally different intents:

1) **Replace policy**  
   “If it’s the same name/path, I want the incoming file to win.”
   - This is what you described: “overwrite for sure”
   - It’s deterministic and avoids leaving “old versions” behind.

2) **Skip policy**  
   “If it already exists, keep what’s there.”
   - This is safer when you want to preserve existing recovered files
   - But it can leave the “wrong” version in place if you’re trying to restore installers/DLLs to what you picked.

## Your overwrite requirement (the most important part)
You said (paraphrasing):  
> “Files match exactly (hex, extension, dates to millisecond, size to the byte). Overwrite for sure.”

That sounds like you’re trying to define overwrite behavior based on content equality and/or metadata equality.

### Two ways to interpret your requirement
**Interpretation A (simple):**  
Always overwrite the destination colliding file with the incoming one.  
- Collision policy = **Replace**
- No comparison needed.

**Interpretation B (verification):**  
Only overwrite if the incoming file differs (or only overwrite when it matches criteria you define).  
That requires comparing the destination file to the incoming one (by hash or by byte comparison).

## Best practice for your goal
Because you explicitly want “overwrite for sure” and also want to avoid duplicates/mess, the most robust approach is:

- **Collision policy = Replace**
- Collision routing (destination numbering) = the file moves into the next available `F:\@MERGE.000X` *for that colliding file*.
- After moving, optionally run a **verification scan**:
  - Compute hashes for duplicates and produce a small report.
  - This doesn’t prevent overwrite; it proves what happened.

## The one question I still need (and why)
When a collision happens at a specific destination path, should the script:

1) **Replace unconditionally** (incoming file replaces existing destination file)
   - This matches your “overwrite for sure”.
2) **Replace only if different** (compare hash/size/mtime and only replace when different)

Reply with **1 or 2**.

If you answer **1**, I’ll keep it fast and deterministic.  
If you answer **2**, I’ll add a comparison step (hashing or byte comparison). Hashing is usually best for speed; if you insist on “bit/byte exact” verification, we can use hashing first and then byte-compare only when hash matches (or reverse).

### Optional: what tools I would use
I can implement hashing natively in PowerShell using built-in .NET (`SHA256`)—no external tools required.
If you later want GUI scanners or advanced diff, we can add open-source tools, but for now PowerShell hashing is enough.

```powershell
#requires -RunAsAdministrator
<#
Recovery/Merge Tool (Windows 11)
- A) Browse to Move:
  1) Renames selected folder to "@MERGE" in-place
  2) Moves its contents into F:\@MERGE
  3) If a destination file already exists (same relative path + same name/ext),
     the incoming colliding file is redirected into the next available F:\@MERGE.000X
     (0001–9999). Non-colliding files stay where they are.
  4) Takes ownership recursively and grants Administrators Full Control ("777-equivalent") recursively.

- B) Browse to Restore:
  This script currently restores at the folder level:
  - If you select a folder under one of the F:\@MERGE* destinations, it will move it back to its parent as "@MERGE"
    by reversing the "contents under @MERGE*" concept.
  - True original filename/extension restoration requires a mapping/log created earlier; not implemented.

USAGE:
1) Run this script as admin
2) Use the popup to choose A or B

NOTE:
- Collision policy is "Keep existing + redirect incoming" (no overwrite).
- Uses file-level collision routing to avoid moving non-colliding files into new folders.
#>

Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing

function Assert-Admin {
    $id = [Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent()
    $p = New-Object Security.Principal.WindowsPrincipal($id)
    if (-not $p.IsInRole([Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltInRole]::Administrator)) {
        throw "Please run this script as Administrator."
    }
}

function Get-NextMergeRoot {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BasePath,   # F:\@MERGE
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$FileRelPath  # used to test existence
    )
    # Ensure BasePath exists
    if (-not (Test-Path $BasePath)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BasePath -Force | Out-Null
    }

    # First try BasePath (F:\@MERGE)
    $candidate = $BasePath
    $destFile = Join-Path $candidate $FileRelPath
    if (-not (Test-Path $destFile)) { return $candidate }

    # Then try @MERGE.0001..@MERGE.9999
    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $suffix = "{0:0000}" -f $i   # 0001..9999
        $cand = "${BasePath}.$suffix"
        $destFile = Join-Path $cand $FileRelPath
        if (-not (Test-Path $destFile)) {
            if (-not (Test-Path $cand)) {
                New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $cand -Force | Out-Null
            }
            return $cand
        }
    }

    throw "Collision routing failed: all F:\@MERGE.0001..@MERGE.9999 already contain $FileRelPath"
}

function TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path
    )

    # Using icacls + takeown for recursive takeover/grant.
    # Grant: Administrators:(F)
    # takeown: /F path /R /D Y
    # icacls: grant Administrators Full Control recursively, replace existing inherited ACLs
    #
    # /T traverses subdirectories/files
    # /C continue on errors
    # /L follow symlinks (optional; leave off to avoid surprises)
    $quoted = $Path.Replace('"','')

    Write-Host "Taking ownership + granting Administrators Full Control for: $Path" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    cmd /c "takeown /F `"$quoted`" /R /D Y" | Out-Null
    cmd /c "icacls `"$quoted`" /inheritance:e /grant:r Administrators:(F) /T /C" | Out-Null
}

function New-DirIfMissing {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path)
    if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $Path -Force | Out-Null
    }
}

function Move-WithFileLevelCollisionRouting {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,   # the renamed local @MERGE folder (in-place)
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot # F:\@MERGE
    )

    New-DirIfMissing $DestBaseRoot

    # Enumerate all files and move them one by one to destinations chosen per collision.
    # Also ensure directories are created under chosen destination roots.
    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force

    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')
        $chosenRoot = Get-NextMergeRoot -BasePath $DestBaseRoot -FileRelPath $rel

        $destDir = Split-Path -Parent (Join-Path $chosenRoot $rel)
        New-DirIfMissing $destDir

        $destPath = Join-Path $chosenRoot $rel

        # Collision policy is "keep existing, redirect incoming"
        # chosenRoot guarantees destPath doesn't exist.
        Write-Host "Moving: $($f.FullName) -> $destPath" -ForegroundColor Green
        Move-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    # After moving files, clean up empty directories under source root
    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}

function Rename-FolderToMergeInPlace {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder
    )
    # Rename selected folder to "@MERGE" in its current parent folder.
    $parent = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $currentName = Split-Path -Leaf $SelectedFolder
    $target = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if ($currentName -ieq "@MERGE") {
        Write-Host "Selected folder is already @MERGE. Continuing." -ForegroundColor Yellow
        return $SelectedFolder
    }

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Cannot rename in-place: $target already exists. Please choose a different folder or move it out of the way."
    }

    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE"
    return $target
}

function BrowseForm-Invoke {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnMove,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnRestore
    )

    $form = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Form
    $form.Text = "Recovery/Merge Tool"
    $form.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(420,170)
    $form.StartPosition = "CenterScreen"
    $form.Topmost = $true

    $btnMove = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Button

    $btnRestore = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Button
    $btnRestore.Location = New-Object System.Drawing.Point(210,20)
    $btnRestore.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(170,40)
    $btnRestore.Text = "B) BROWSE TO RESTORE"

    $lbl = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Label
    $lbl.Location = New-Object System.Drawing.Point(20,75)
    $lbl.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(360,50)
    $lbl.Text = "Move: rename to @MERGE then merge contents into F:\@MERGE*"
    $lbl.AutoSize = $false

    $form.Controls.Add($btnRestore)
    $form.Controls.Add($lbl)

    $folderDialog = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.FolderBrowserDialog
    $folderDialog.ShowNewFolderButton = $false
    $folderDialog.Description = "Select a folder."

    $btnMove.Add_Click({
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select the folder to MOVE (it will be renamed to @MERGE in-place)."
        $res = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()

        $OnMove.Invoke($folderDialog.SelectedPath)
    })

    $btnRestore.Add_Click({
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select a folder under F:\@MERGE* to RESTORE (folder-level action)."
        $res = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()
        if ($res -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }
        $OnRestore.Invoke($folderDialog.SelectedPath)
    })

    [void]$form.ShowDialog()
}

function Restore-FolderLevel {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder
    )
    # This is a placeholder "folder-level undo" that moves the selected folder
    # back under its parent as @MERGE (best-effort).
    #
    # True original per-file name/extension restoration requires a mapping/log created earlier.
    $parent = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $target = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Target already exists: $target"
    }

    Write-Host "Renaming $SelectedFolder -> $target (folder-level restore only)" -ForegroundColor Cyan
    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE" -ErrorAction Stop

    TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $target
    Write-Host "Restore complete (folder-level)." -ForegroundColor Green
}

BrowseForm-Invoke `

        if (Test-Path $destBase) { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destBase }
```

```powershell
#requires -RunAsAdministrator
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Drawing

function Assert-Admin {
    $id = [Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent()
    $p  = New-Object Security.Principal.WindowsPrincipal($id)
    if (-not $p.IsInRole([Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltInRole]::Administrator)) {
        throw "Please run this script as Administrator."
    }
}

function New-SessionLogRoot {
    # Creates: F:\@MERGE.LOG.0001 .. F:\@MERGE.LOG.9999 (first available)
    # Inside:   a .txt file named:
    #            timestamp_old.202608200754595959.TXT
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BaseDestRoot,   # e.g. F:\
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Prefix           # e.g. "@MERGE.LOG"
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $BaseDestRoot)) { throw "Destination base root not found: $BaseDestRoot" }

    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $suffix = "{0:0000}" -f $i  # 0001..9999
        $dir = Join-Path $BaseDestRoot "${Prefix}.$suffix"
        if (-not (Test-Path $dir)) {
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $dir -Force | Out-Null
            $stampOld = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("yyyyMMddHHmmssfffffff") # UTC with high precision
            # Example requested: OLD.202608200754595959.TXT (you had 17 digits; this is precise/monotonic-like)
            $logFile = Join-Path $dir ("OLD.$stampOld.TXT")
            $null = New-Item -ItemType File -Path $logFile -Force
            return [pscustomobject]@{
                SessionDir = $dir
                LogFile    = $logFile
                SessionId   = $suffix
                StampOld    = $stampOld
            }
        }
    }

    throw "Could not create new session log root: all ${Prefix}.0001..${Prefix}.9999 exist."
}

function Append-LogLine {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Line
    )
    $Line = $Line.Replace("`r","").Replace("`n","")
    Add-Content -Path $LogFile -Value $Line -Encoding UTF8
}

function Get-NextMergeRoot {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$BasePath,   # F:\@MERGE
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$FileRelPath  # used to test existence
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $BasePath)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $BasePath -Force | Out-Null
    }

    $candidate = $BasePath
    $destFile  = Join-Path $candidate $FileRelPath
    if (-not (Test-Path $destFile)) { return $candidate }

    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $suffix   = "{0:0000}" -f $i   # 0001..9999
        $cand     = "${BasePath}.$suffix"
        $destFile = Join-Path $cand $FileRelPath
        if (-not (Test-Path $destFile)) {
            if (-not (Test-Path $cand)) {
                New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $cand -Force | Out-Null
            }
            return $cand
        }
    }

    throw "Collision routing failed: all F:\@MERGE.0001..@MERGE.9999 already contain $FileRelPath"
}

function TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path
    )
    $quoted = $Path.Replace('"','')

    cmd /c "takeown /F `"$quoted`" /R /D Y" | Out-Null
    cmd /c "icacls `"$quoted`" /inheritance:e /grant:r Administrators:(F) /T /C" | Out-Null
}

function New-DirIfMissing {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Path)
    if (-not (Test-Path $Path)) {
        New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $Path -Force | Out-Null
    }
}

function Move-WithFileLevelCollisionRouting {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,   # in-place renamed @MERGE folder
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot, # F:\@MERGE
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile
    )

    New-DirIfMissing $DestBaseRoot

    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force

    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')
        $chosenRoot = Get-NextMergeRoot -BasePath $DestBaseRoot -FileRelPath $rel

        $destPath = Join-Path $chosenRoot $rel
        $destDir  = Split-Path -Parent $destPath
        New-DirIfMissing $destDir

        # Collision policy: keep existing; redirect incoming (ensured by Get-NextMergeRoot)
        $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")

        Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("MOVE_OLDPATH={0}|NEW_PATH={1}|UTC={2}" -f $f.FullName, $destPath, $ts)

        Move-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    # Clean up empty directories under source root
    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}

function Rename-FolderToMergeInPlace {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder)

    $parent       = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $currentName  = Split-Path -Leaf $SelectedFolder
    $target       = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if ($currentName -ieq "@MERGE") { return $SelectedFolder }

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Cannot rename in-place: $target already exists. Please choose a different folder or move it out of the way."
    }

    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE"
    return $target
}
```

```powershell
function New-SessionLogRoot {
    <#
      Creates:
        F:\@MERGE.LOG.0001 .. F:\@MERGE.LOG.9999 (first free)
      Inside session folder:
        OLD.<UTC_HIGH_PREC>.TXT
    #>
    param(

        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Prefix           # e.g. @MERGE.LOG
    )

    if (-not (Test-Path $BaseDestRoot)) { throw "Destination base root not found: $BaseDestRoot" }

    for ($i=1; $i -le 9999; $i++) {
        $suffix = "{0:0000}" -f $i  # 0001..9999
        $dir = Join-Path $BaseDestRoot ("${Prefix}.$suffix")
        if (-not (Test-Path $dir)) {
            New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $dir -Force | Out-Null

            # UTC high precision stamp (ticks). Example format like your request:
            # OLD.202608200754595959.TXT  (script produces a consistent high-precision string)
            $stampOld = (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("yyyyMMddHHmmssfffffff").Trim()
            $logFile = Join-Path $dir ("OLD.$stampOld.TXT")

            New-Item -ItemType File -Path $logFile -Force | Out-Null

                SessionId  = $suffix
                StampOld   = $stampOld

function Move-WithFileLevelCollisionRouting {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SourceRoot,    # in-place renamed @MERGE folder
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$DestBaseRoot, # F:\@MERGE
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$LogFile
    )

    New-DirIfMissing $DestBaseRoot

    $files = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -File -Force

    foreach ($f in $files) {
        $rel = $f.FullName.Substring($SourceRoot.Length).TrimStart('\')
        $chosenRoot = Get-NextMergeRoot -BasePath $DestBaseRoot -FileRelPath $rel

        $destPath = Join-Path $chosenRoot $rel
        $destDir  = Split-Path -Parent $destPath
        New-DirIfMissing $destDir

        # Log old -> new (and UTC timestamp for traceability)
        $ts = (Get-Date).ToString("yyyy-MM-ddTHH:mm:ss.fffffff")
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $LogFile -Line ("MOVE_OLDPATH={0}|NEW_PATH={1}|UTC={2}" -f $f.FullName, $destPath, $ts)

        # Keep existing; redirect incoming by choosing a root where the destination file doesn't exist.
        Move-Item -LiteralPath $f.FullName -Destination $destPath -Force
    }

    # Best-effort cleanup: remove empty directories beneath source root
    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $SourceRoot -Recurse -Directory -Force |
        Sort-Object FullName -Descending |
        ForEach-Object {
            try {
                if ((Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force | Measure-Object).Count -eq 0) {
                    Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
                }
            } catch {}
        }
}

function Rename-FolderToMergeInPlace {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder)

    $parent      = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $currentName = Split-Path -Leaf $SelectedFolder
    $target      = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if ($currentName -ieq "@MERGE") { return $SelectedFolder }

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Cannot rename in-place: $target already exists. Please choose a different folder or move it out of the way."
    }

    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE"
    return $target
}

function BrowseForm-Invoke {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnMove,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnRestore
    )

    $form = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Form
    $form.Text = "Recovery/Merge Tool"
    $form.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(420,170)
    $form.StartPosition = "CenterScreen"
    $form.Topmost = $true

    $btnMove = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Button
    $btnMove.Location = New-Object System.Drawing.Point(20,20)
    $btnMove.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(170,40)
    $btnMove.Text = "A) BROWSE TO MOVE"

    $btnRestore = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Button
    $btnRestore.Location = New-Object System.Drawing.Point(210,20)
    $btnRestore.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(170,40)
    $btnRestore.Text = "B) BROWSE TO RESTORE"

    $lbl = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.Label
    $lbl.Location = New-Object System.Drawing.Point(20,75)
    $lbl.Size = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(360,50)
    $lbl.Text = "Move: rename to @MERGE then merge contents into F:\@MERGE*"
    $lbl.AutoSize = $false

    $form.Controls.Add($btnMove)
    $form.Controls.Add($btnRestore)
    $form.Controls.Add($lbl)

    $folderDialog = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.FolderBrowserDialog
    $folderDialog.ShowNewFolderButton = $false

    $btnMove.Add_Click({
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select the folder to MOVE (it will be renamed to @MERGE in-place)."
        $res = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()
        if ($res -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }
        $OnMove.Invoke($folderDialog.SelectedPath)
    })

    $btnRestore.Add_Click({
        $folderDialog.Description = "Select a folder under F:\@MERGE* to RESTORE (folder-level action)."
        $res = $folderDialog.ShowDialog()
        if ($res -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }
        $OnRestore.Invoke($folderDialog.SelectedPath)
    })

    [void]$form.ShowDialog()
}

function Restore-FolderLevel {
    param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$SelectedFolder)

    # Best-effort: renames the selected folder to "@MERGE" in-place.
    $parent = Split-Path -Parent $SelectedFolder
    $target = Join-Path $parent "@MERGE"

    if (Test-Path $target) {
        throw "Target already exists: $target"
    }

    Rename-Item -LiteralPath $SelectedFolder -NewName "@MERGE" -ErrorAction Stop
    TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $target
}

# ---------------- MAIN ----------------
Assert-Admin

$destBase = "F:\@MERGE"
$session = New-SessionLogRoot -BaseDestRoot "F:\" -Prefix "@MERGE.LOG"

Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("SESSION_START|SessionDir={0}|LogFile={1}|UTC={2}" -f $session.SessionDir, $session.LogFile, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))
Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("SESSION_INPUT|StartUser={0}|Computer={1}|UTC={2}" -f $env:USERNAME, $env:COMPUTERNAME, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

BrowseForm-Invoke `
    -OnMove {
        param($selected)

        $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MOVE_SESSION_REQUESTED|Selected={0}|UTC={1}" -f $selected, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        $renamedRoot = Rename-FolderToMergeInPlace -SelectedFolder $selected
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("RENAME_ROOT|OldRoot={0}|NewRoot={1}|UTC={2}" -f $selected, $renamedRoot, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        # Take ownership + grant admins before heavy operations (more reliable)
        TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $renamedRoot

        # Move with file-level collision routing + log per file
        Move-WithFileLevelCollisionRouting -SourceRoot $renamedRoot -DestBaseRoot $destBase -LogFile $session.LogFile

        # Take ownership + grant admins on base and any suffix roots that exist
        if (Test-Path $destBase) { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $destBase }

        $parentOfBase = Split-Path -Parent $destBase
        $suffixRoots = Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $parentOfBase -Directory -Force |
            Where-Object { $_.Name -match '^@MERGE\.\d{4}$' }

        foreach ($r in $suffixRoots) {
            if (Test-Path $r.FullName) { TakeOwnership-And-GrantAdmins -Path $r.FullName }
        }

        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("MOVE_SESSION_COMPLETE|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show(
            "Move/Merge complete.`nLog: $($session.LogFile)",
            "Done",
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxButtons]::OK,
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon]::Information
        ) | Out-Null
    } `
    -OnRestore {
        param($selectedRestoreFolder)

        $ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("RESTORE_SESSION_REQUESTED|Selected={0}|UTC={1}" -f $selectedRestoreFolder, (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        Restore-FolderLevel -SelectedFolder $selectedRestoreFolder

        Append-LogLine -LogFile $session.LogFile -Line ("RESTORE_SESSION_COMPLETE|UTC={0}" -f (Get-Date).ToUniversalTime().ToString("o"))

        [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBox]::Show(
            "Restore complete.`nLog: $($session.LogFile)",
            "Done",
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxButtons]::OK,
            [System.Windows.Forms.MessageBoxIcon]::Information
        ) | Out-Null
    }
```

    if (-not $p.IsInRole([System.Security.Principal.WindowsBuiltInRole]::Administrator)) {

# ------------------ WINFORMS HELPERS ------------------
function Set-FormLocked666 {
    param([System.Windows.Forms.Form]$form)

    $form.StartPosition = 'CenterScreen'
    $form.Text = "DR.APPSTEIN"
    $form.TopMost = $true   # <-- single toggle line (comment/uncomment as desired)

    # Exact 666x666
    $form.ClientSize = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(666, 666)

    # Lock normal resize, but allow maximize/fullscreen behavior via system chrome
    $form.FormBorderStyle = 'FixedSingle'
    $form.MaximizeBox = $true
    $form.MinimizeBox = $true

    $form.MinimumSize = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(666, 666)
    $form.MaximumSize = New-Object System.Drawing.Size(666, 666)
}

function Run-Background {
    param(
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$Work,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnUiStart,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnUiDone,
        [Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][scriptblock]$OnUiError
    )

    $form = $global:WIN_FORM
    $form.BeginInvoke($OnUiStart) | Out-Null

    [void][System.Threading.Tasks.Task]::Run([Action]{
        try {
            & $Work
            $form.BeginInvoke($OnUiDone) | Out-Null
        } catch {
            $msg = $_.Exception.Message
            $form.BeginInvoke([Action]{
                & $OnUiError $msg
            }) | Out-Null
        }
    })
}

$btnRun.Add_Click({
    $drive = $txtDrive.Text.Trim()
    if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($drive)) { $drive = "C" }
    $drive = $drive.ToUpper()
    if ($drive.Length -eq 1) { $drive = "$drive`:" }

    $doSfc = $cbSfc.Checked
    $doDism = $cbDism.Checked
    $doChkdsk = $cbChkdsk.Checked
    $doSysInfo = $cbSysInfo.Checked

    if (-not ($doSfc -or $doDism -or $doChkdsk -or $doSysInfo)) {
        $global:STATUS.Text = "Select at least one tool."
        return
    }

    $modeRepair = $rbRepair.Checked

    $btnRun.Enabled = $false
    $txtStatus.Clear()
    $global:STATUS.Text = "Running..."

    Run-Background `
        -OnUiStart {
            $global:STATUS.Text = "Running selected tools..."
            Append-UIStatus "Start: $(Get-Date -Format o)"
        } `
        -OnUiDone {
            Append-UIStatus "Done: $(Get-Date -Format o)"
            $global:STATUS.Text = "Completed."
            $btnRun.Enabled = $true
        } `
        -OnUiError {
            param($err)
            Append-UIStatus "ERROR: $err"
            $global:STATUS.Text = "Failed."
            $btnRun.Enabled = $true
        } `
        -Work {
            if ($doSfc) {
                Append-UIStatus "SFC..."
                Start-Process -FilePath "sfc.exe" -ArgumentList "/SCANNOW" -NoNewWindow -Wait | Out-Null
            }
            if ($doDism) {
                Append-UIStatus "DISM..."
                Start-Process -FilePath "DISM.exe" -ArgumentList "/Online","/Cleanup-Image","/RestoreHealth" -NoNewWindow -Wait | Out-Null
            }
            if ($doChkdsk) {
                Append-UIStatus "CHKDSK on $drive..."
                if ($modeRepair) {
                    Start-Process -FilePath "chkdsk.exe" -ArgumentList "$drive","/F" -NoNewWindow -Wait | Out-Null
                } else {
                    Start-Process -FilePath "chkdsk.exe" -ArgumentList "$drive" -NoNewWindow -Wait | Out-Null
                }
            }
            if ($doSysInfo) {
                Append-UIStatus "SYSTEMINFO..."
                $out = & systeminfo 2>&1 | Out-String
                Append-UIStatus ($out.TrimEnd())
            }
        }
})

        $matrixGroup.Controls.Add($b) | Out-Null
    }
